### [1] [Asymmetric Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23889)
*Vijay Kumar,Ramakrishna Bandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了具有非对称局部性的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码(AEA-qLRC),允许针对X型和Z型错误分别设置不同的局部恢复参数{r_X, r_Z},突破了现有量子局部可恢复码对两类错误使用单一局部性参数的限制,并建立了相应的理论界限和最优性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子局部可恢复码(qLRC)及其纠缠辅助版本对比特翻转(X型)错误和相位翻转(Z型)错误采用单一的局部性参数r进行恢复,但实际物理噪声在这两种错误类型之间往往是不对称的。为了更好地适应真实噪声环境并优化编码性能,需要开发允许对X型和Z型错误分别设置不同局部恢复参数的量子编码方案。

Method: 1) 引入非对称局部性概念,定义具有参数{r_X, r_Z}的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码,使X型错误和Z型错误分别从最多r_X+1和r_Z+1大小的恢复集中纠正;2) 扩展稳定子充分条件,特别是CSS框架,将非对称局部性对应到构造中两个经典码C_1和C_2各自的局部性条件;3) 基于该刻画推导出多种类型的理论界限,包括Singleton型、Griesmer型、Plotkin型、球填充型和Cadambe-Mazumdar型界限;4) 精确刻画由经典局部可恢复码对构建的纯CSS型AEA-qLRC何时能达到Singleton型界限的等式。

Result: 成功建立了CSS型非对称纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码的理论框架,推导出多个基本界限(Singleton型、Griesmer型、Plotkin型、球填充型、Cadambe-Mazumdar型),并给出了达到Singleton型界限等式的充要条件。该框架允许针对X型和Z型错误的非对称特性分别优化局部恢复参数,为实际量子存储系统提供了更灵活的编码设计方案。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入非对称局部性参数{r_X, r_Z},突破了量子局部可恢复码对称设计的限制,建立了完整的AEA-qLRC理论体系,包括CSS构造框架、多种基本界限及最优性刻画。这一工作为量子分布式存储和容错量子存储器在面对非对称物理噪声时提供了更高效、更灵活的编码方案,具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: Locally recoverable codes (LRCs) permit reconstruction of a lost coordinate from a small subset of the remaining coordinates, a property that is a fundamental coding technique for distributed storage and, more recently, for fault-tolerant quantum memories and distributed storage systems. Existing constructions of quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) and their entanglement-assisted generalizations (EA-qLRCs) impose a single locality parameter $r$ governing recovery from both bit-flip ($X$-type) and phase-flip ($Z$-type) errors, even though physical noise is rarely symmetric between the two. We introduce entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes (EA-qLRC) with \emph{asymmetric locality} $\{r_X,r_Z\}$, in which $X$-type and $Z$-type errors are corrected from recovery sets of size at most $r_X+1$ and $r_Z+1$, respectively. We extend a stabilizer sufficient condition for this property and, in particular, the CSS framework, where asymmetric locality corresponds to separate locality conditions on the two classical codes $\mathcal C_1$ and $\mathcal C_2$ underlying the construction. From this characterization we derive Singleton-, Griesmer-, Plotkin-, sphere-packing-, and Cadambe\textendash Mazumdar-like bounds for CSS-type asymmetric EA-qLRCs (AEA-qLRCs) built from Proposition~\ref{CSS-EA-qLRC-Asym}, and we characterize exactly when a pure CSS-type AEA-qLRC built from a pair of classical LRCs attains the Singleton-like bound with equality.

</details>


### [2] [Designing Robust Quantum Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24039)
*Yuanxing Hao,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了d能级量子系统中鲁棒量子门实现的存在性准则，证明了当且仅当驱动哈密顿量不共享对称性时才存在通用鲁棒门族，并通过数值方法构造了可将门保真度误差降低2-7个数量级的显式鲁棒控制协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子控制理论只能判断量子门能否实现，但无法评估其抗误差能力。实际量子系统面临静态扰动、哈密顿量重整化、特定频率噪声和环境诱导噪声等多种误差源，亟需建立理论框架来判断何时可以设计出对这些误差具有鲁棒性的量子门，并提供实用的构造方法。

Method: 1) 理论分析：建立d能级系统中鲁棒量子门存在的充要条件，证明驱动哈密顿量无共同对称性是实现通用鲁棒门族的关键；2) 鲁棒性设计：针对多种误差类型（静态扰动、均匀哈密顿量重整化、特定频率扰动、任意选定波形扰动）建立鲁棒性条件，可根据有限误差信息（如仅需噪声峰值频率而无需完整频谱细节）灵活定制；3) 数值构造：开发显式数值协议来实现满足鲁棒性条件的量子门控制序列。

Result: 数值实验表明，构造的鲁棒控制协议在哈密顿量扰动和环境诱导噪声下，可将平均门不保真度降低约2至7个数量级。存在性准则为评估量子平台潜力提供了理论判据，数值构造方法可直接应用于实际鲁棒量子门设计。

Conclusion: 本研究通过建立鲁棒量子门的存在性准则和数值构造方法，将量子控制理论从可实现性扩展到鲁棒性层面。核心发现是驱动哈密顿量的对称性破缺是实现通用鲁棒门的充要条件。该框架具有高度实用性：可适应不同误差类型，仅需有限误差信息，且显著提升门操作保真度，为量子平台评估和鲁棒量子门设计提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: For given control channels, quantum control theory determines whether quantum gates can be realized, but not their resilience to errors. We address this problem by establishing existence criteria for robust gate implementation in $d$-level systems. Our criteria reveal that implementability of all gates is not enough: a universal family of robust gates exists if and only if the driven Hamiltonians share no common symmetry. When these criteria hold, we can make gates resilient to any combination of static perturbations, uniform Hamiltonian rescaling, perturbations at a finite set of selected frequencies, and perturbations with arbitrary selected waveforms. We can impose robustness conditions broadly with limited information about errors, e.g., against any static perturbations; we can also tailor these conditions to the platform, e.g., against environment-induced noise with dominant spectral peaks, requiring no spectral details beyond the peak frequencies. We numerically construct explicit protocols for robust gates, reducing average gate infidelity by approximately two to seven orders of magnitude under Hamiltonian perturbations and environment-induced noise. Together, our existence criteria and numerical constructions provide practical guidance for evaluating the potential of quantum platforms and designing robust quantum gates.

</details>


### [3] [Operational Meaning of the Mandelstam-Tamm Time Scale in a Spin-1/2 Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24056)
*Jakub,Rog*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Georgiev关于自由进动自旋的分析提出不同观点,证明Mandelstam-Tamm量ΔT在常规工作点等于经典Fisher信息的倒数平方根,其在极值点的发散反映的是所选读出方式局部灵敏度的丧失而非物理时间的无限方差,从而论证ΔT具有物理意义并能揭示系统测量时间流逝的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Georgiev分析了自由进动自旋系统,认为Mandelstam-Tamm量ΔT在时钟信号极值点附近的发散缺乏物理意义,且ΔT不能揭示系统如何测量时间流逝。作者旨在反驳这一结论,证明ΔT实际上具有明确的物理意义,并能够量化系统作为时钟的性能。

Method: 作者采用量子参数估计理论中的经典Fisher信息作为分析工具,针对相同的投影测量σ̂_x读出方式,在常规工作点建立了ΔT与单次二元测量结果的经典Fisher信息平方根倒数之间的等价关系。通过分析极值点处的发散行为,区分了时钟的滴答周期(确定性)与定位单次滴答的统计精度(不确定性),并考察了互补测量σ̂_y在极值点的信息贡献。

Result: 研究表明,在常规工作点,ΔT等于单次二元测量结果经典Fisher信息的倒数平方根。ΔT在次最大相干性极值点的发散识别出所选读出方式局部灵敏度的丧失,而非物理时间的无限方差。在这些极值点,互补的σ̂_y测量仍然保持信息性。该解释成功地将确定性的滴答周期与定位单个滴答的统计精度区分开来。

Conclusion: 作者部分同意Georgiev对时间算符解释的警告,但否定了其关于Mandelstam-Tamm尺度缺乏物理内容的强结论。ΔT的发散具有明确的物理意义,它表征的是特定测量方案局部灵敏度的退化,而非时间测量本身的失效。通过Fisher信息的诠释,ΔT能够有效地揭示量子系统作为时钟测量时间流逝的能力,具有重要的物理内容和实际意义。

Abstract: Georgiev's analysis of a freely precessing spin correctly emphasizes that the Mandelstam-Tamm quantity $ΔT=Δ\hat A/|d\langle\hat A\rangle/d t|$ is not the variance of a time operator. He concludes, however, that its divergence near the extrema of the clock signal is devoid of physical meaning and that $ΔT$ does not disclose how well the system measures the passage of time. For the same projective $\hatσ_x$ readout, we show that $ΔT$ equals the inverse square root of the classical Fisher information in one binary outcome at regular operating points. Its divergence at submaximal-coherence extrema therefore identifies a loss of local sensitivity in the selected readout, not an infinite variance of physical time; a complementary $\hatσ_y$ measurement remains informative. This interpretation also separates Georgiev's exactly known tick--tock period from the statistical precision with which a tick can be located. It preserves his warning against a time-operator interpretation while qualifying his stronger conclusion that the Mandelstam--Tamm scale lacks physical content.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum error correction well below the color code threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24077)
*Ali Lavasani,Adam P. Reed,Joan M. Dreiling,Annie J. Park,Michael Mills,Matteo Puviani,Gabriel Matos,M. S. Allman,Noah Berthusen,Natalie C. Brown,Victor E. Colussi,Brian Estey,Cameron Foltz,Daniel Gresh,Reza Haghshenas,Akhil Isanaka,Nikhil Kotibhaskar,Anthony Ransford,Ciarán Ryan-Anderson,Brian Neyenhuis,David Hayes,Tzvetan S. Metodi,Andrew C. Potter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在Quantinuum的Helios离子阱量子处理器上演示了距离d=3,5,7的三角形色码的低于阈值运行，实现了5×10^-6的超低逻辑错误率，随码距增加错误抑制超过一个数量级(Λ=12)，并通过神经网络解码器实现了实时解码，展示了离子阱量子计算机向可扩展容错量子计算迈进的重要进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错是实现大规模容错量子计算的关键技术，但需要物理错误率降至临界阈值以下才能实现逻辑错误的指数级抑制。尽管已有理论进展，但在实际量子硬件上演示低于阈值的操作并实现显著的错误抑制仍然是一个重大挑战。此外，需要验证离子阱量子处理器是否能以比其他量子比特模态更小的编码块达到应用级性能，同时实现快速的实时解码。

Method: 1) 在Quantinuum的Helios离子阱量子处理器上实现距离d=3,5,7的三角形色码；2) 采用电路级和硬件级泄漏抑制方案降低错误率；3) 对编码在d=3,5色码中的Clifford双量子比特随机基准测试电路进行低于阈值的错误率缩放验证；4) 比较现有近最优算法解码器与基于卷积神经网络(CNN)和Transformer的神经网络解码器的逻辑性能；5) 验证神经网络解码器能够以比校验子提取速率快一个数量级的速度实现实时解码。

Result: 1) 实现了低至5×10^-6的逻辑错误率；2) 随着码距的每次增加，错误抑制超过一个数量级，抑制因子达到Λ=12；3) 在距离5的色码中，每个逻辑双量子比特Clifford操作的逻辑错误率为7×10^-5，抑制因子为Λ=9；4) 成功演示了Clifford双量子比特随机基准测试电路在d=3,5色码编码下的低于阈值错误率缩放；5) 神经网络解码器实现了比校验子提取速率快一个数量级的实时解码。

Conclusion: 本研究成功在离子阱量子处理器上实现了低于阈值的量子纠错操作，达到了接近百万量子操作(megaquop)规模量子计算所需的逻辑错误率水平。结果表明，扩展的离子阱处理器可以用比其他量子比特模态先前结果所示更小的编码块达到应用相关的性能水平。神经网络解码器的实时解码能力进一步增强了系统的实用性。这些成果标志着向可扩展、容错量子计算迈出了重要一步，为未来大规模量子计算应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum error correction enables scalable fault-tolerant quantum computation by suppressing logical errors exponentially with code distance once physical error rates fall below a critical threshold. Here we demonstrate below-threshold operation of triangular color codes with distances $d=3,5,$ and $7$ on Quantinuum's Helios trapped-ion quantum processor. Leveraging both circuit- and hardware- level leakage reduction schemes, we achieve logical error rates as low as $5_{-2}^{+4}\times 10^{-6}$, and demonstrate error suppression by over an order of magnitude, $Λ=12_{-2}^{+3}$, with each successive increase in code distance. Moreover, we demonstrate below threshold scaling of error rates for Clifford two-qubit randomized benchmarking circuits when encoded in $d=3,5$ color codes, achieving logical error rate of $7^{+3}_{-3}\times 10^{-5}$ per logical two-qubit Clifford at distance $5$ with a suppression factor of $Λ=9_{-3}^{+8}$. We compare logical performance for existing near-optimal algorithmic decoders, and both convolutional neural network and transformer-based neural network decoders capable of real-time decoding an order of magnitude faster than the syndrome extraction rate. These results approach the logical error rates required for megaquop-scale quantum computation and indicate that scaled trapped-ion processors could reach application-relevant performance with substantially smaller code blocks than indicated by previous results in other qubit modalities.

</details>


### [5] [Performance of a Hot Rb Vapour-Based Portable Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24098)
*Kenneth Gregory,Konrad Socha,Khaled Mnaymneh,Connor Kupchak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于铷原子温蒸气的商用量子存储器在量子中继器中的应用性能,为其集成到现有光通信架构中建立了性能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子互联网的实现需要量子中继器来克服长距离光纤传输中的损耗问题。有效的中继节点需要至少两个量子存储器单元,因此需要开发可扩展、便携、鲁棒且低开销的量子存储器。然而,目前缺乏将量子存储器性能指标与现有光通信协议标准统一的研究,这阻碍了量子存储器系统无缝集成到当前通信架构中。

Method: 本研究使用基于铷原子温蒸气的商用量子存储器作为研究对象,在一系列可控的蒸气温度范围内对该设备进行系统表征。具体测量并分析了四个关键性能指标:相干时间(coherence time)、绝对效率(absolute efficiency)、信噪比(signal to noise ratio)和误码率(bit error ratio)。重要的是,研究将这些性能指标转换为与当前光通信协议相同的标准约定,以便进行直接比较和评估。

Result: 研究成功表征了商用铷原子温蒸气量子存储器在不同工作温度下的性能参数,包括相干时间、绝对效率、信噪比和误码率。这些性能指标被统一转换到现有光通信协议的标准框架下,为评估该量子存储器在实际量子中继网络中的可行性提供了量化数据。研究结果表明该设备的性能随蒸气温度变化呈现可控的规律。

Conclusion: 本研究为基于温蒸气的商用量子存储器建立了符合光通信协议标准的性能评估体系,这是将量子存储器系统无缝集成到现有通信架构中的关键步骤。开发满足这些基准的量子存储器系统对于未来量子互联网和量子中继网络的实际部署至关重要。该研究证明了商用温蒸气量子存储器作为可扩展、实用化量子中继节点的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum repeaters will be paramount to the success of the future quantum internet. Presently however, the losses incurred in fibre communications are unsustainable when fragile quantum states are transmitted over long distances. The most effective repeater nodes will require incorporation of a minimum of two quantum memory units (QMems) for successful operation. Therefore a network composed of numerous nodes will ideally require scalable repeaters that contain QMems that are portable, robust and do not require a high amount of overhead. Here we investigate the performance of a commercial QMem based on a warm vapour of Rb atoms. Specifically, we characterize the coherence time, absolute efficiency, signal to noise ratio and bit error ratio of this device over a range of vapour temperatures that are readily controllable. For our system, we place these performance metrics into the same conventions as those established for current optical communication protocols. Development of QMem systems meeting these benchmarks will be a vital step for seamless integration of QMems systems into present-day communication architectures.

</details>


### [6] [Translation-Invariant Pure States with Exotic Half-Chain Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24154)
*Ruizhi Liu,Jinmin Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在一维晶格上构造了具有II_∞型或III_λ型超有限因子的平移不变纯准自由费米子态,证明了半链冯·诺依曼代数的因子类型与纠缠熵增长率之间没有确定性关联,并揭示了规范不变二次哈密顿量基态的二分法性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为量子自旋链的半链冯·诺依曼代数通常是I型或III型因子,且纠缠熵的标度行为可能决定因子类型。本文旨在挑战这一认知,探索是否存在具有II_∞型或其他非常规III_λ型因子的平移不变纯态,以及纠缠熵增长与因子类型之间的真实关系。

Method: 通过在一维晶格上构造平移不变的纯准自由费米子态,利用Jordan-Wigner-Araki变换将其映射为自旋链态。这些态是具有超多项式衰减相互作用的无能隙平移不变母哈密顿量的唯一基态。系统地研究不同因子类型(II_∞和III_λ,0<λ≤1)的实现,并分析相应的区间纠缠熵增长行为。对规范不变的二次哈密顿量进行理论证明,建立其基态性质的二分法定理。

Result: 成功构造了半链冯·诺依曼代数为II_∞型或任意III_λ型(0<λ≤1)超有限因子的平移不变纯态,证明了这类因子不必局限于I型或III型。对于固定的因子类型,区间纠缠熵可以呈现从任意缓慢发散到接近幂律增长的广泛变化,表明熵标度不能决定因子类型。对于具有有限味数和指数衰减跳跃的规范不变二次哈密顿量,证明了其基态满足二分法:要么满足面积定律且半链因子为I型,要么具有对数熵增长且因子为III_1型。

Conclusion: 本文打破了半链因子类型与纠缠熵标度之间存在确定性关联的传统认知,展示了量子多体系统中更丰富的数学结构。证明了II_∞型和非常规III_λ型因子可以出现在物理相关的平移不变纯态中,并为规范不变二次系统建立了严格的二分法定理。这些结果深化了对量子纠缠、冯·诺依曼代数分类及其在凝聚态物理中应用的理解,也展示了AI辅助在数学物理反例发现中的潜力。

Abstract: We construct translation-invariant pure quasifree fermionic states on the one-dimensional lattice whose half-chain von Neumann algebras are hyperfinite factors of type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ or $\mathrm{III}_λ$ for any $0<λ\leq 1$. Via the Jordan-Wigner-Araki transformation, these yield translation-invariant pure spin-chain states with the same half-chain factors, demonstrating that such factors need not be of type I or III. Each state is the unique ground state of a gapless, translation-invariant parent Hamiltonian with superpolynomially decaying interactions. For any fixed factor type, the interval entanglement entropy can exhibit widely varying growth rates, from arbitrarily slow divergence to nearly power-law growth, showing that entropy scaling does not determine the factor type. In contrast, we prove that gauge-invariant quadratic Hamiltonians with finitely many flavours and exponentially decaying hopping exhibit a dichotomy: their ground states either satisfy an area law with a type I half-chain factor, or have logarithmic entropy growth with a type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factor. The counterexample was discovered with AI assistance.

Abstract: Benchmarking quantum processors is a central task in every roadmap aimed at quantum advantage. We compare it with Mermin's inequality, and present the results of applying this framework to two different quantum processing units -the superconducting system Garnet (from IQM) and the ion-trap system Marmot (from AQT), testing different families of quantum circuits for three to five qubits under identical experimental conditions. We also run simultaneous triplet tests across the full register, including a comparison between GHZ and linear cluster states, to probe how Svetlichny-inequality violations depend on qubit location and calibration. Our results demonstrate that tests based on genuine multipartite nonlocality provide a powerful tool to characterize the collective performance of quantum devices and to guide their calibration and optimization.

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires high-fidelity quantum gates that are resilient to control errors and device imperfections. For a gate duration of 90ns, we achieve fidelities above 99.9% for the X gate and 99.6% for Hadamard-type gates. For the X gate, we maintain fidelities above 99.5% for simultaneous drive-amplitude variations up to 20% and qubit detuning up to 4 MHz. This robustness outperforms that of conventional Gaussian pulses by an order of magnitude, without much difference in peak gate fidelity. These results establish a route towards robust single-qubit control in large-scale quantum processors, advancing the development of fault-tolerant quantum computing.

Both trapped-ion processors sustain violations up to ten qubits, while the superconducting processor loses them beyond six qubits under unmitigated (raw) conditions; readout-error mitigation recovers violations up to eight qubits, with a substantial fraction of the degradation attributable to measurement imperfections. These processors provide a heterogeneous testbed for validating the protocol's cross-platform applicability and discriminating power, demonstrating its practicality as a common framework for assessing collective quantum performance.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单次求解方案需要极高的量子态保真度(低于10^-7)才能有效求解不可压缩流动问题中的压力-泊松方程，这在当前有噪声的量子硬件上难以实现。为了降低对量子硬件精度的要求，使其能够在现有的中等规模含噪量子(NISQ)设备上实际应用，需要开发一种对量子噪声更具鲁棒性的求解策略。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典迭代细化方法成功消除了单次求解方案对超高量子态保真度的严格要求，使得在当前有噪声的量子硬件上求解不可压缩流动问题的压力-泊松方程成为可能。实验结果表明，该方法在多个量子处理器上均能达到高精度(残差低于10^-7)，证明了其在NISQ时代实际应用的可行性，为量子计算在计算流体力学领域的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical iterative refinement for the pressure-Poisson equation of the incompressible Navier-Stokes projection method on noisy quantum hardware. Rather than solving the pressure equation once per time step, we solve it repeatedly for the normalized residual and scale each quantum direction by a least-squares fit to the physical residual. As a result, the error of the quantum solve enters as a contraction factor $\sinθ_k$ of the residual rather than as an error floor. This removes the requirement of a state infidelity below $10^{-7}$ that was estimated for single-solve schemes. With a boundary-consistent staggered (MAC) discretization, the pressure residual equals the velocity divergence left after projection up to the factor $Δt/ρ$. On the IBM processor ibm_cleveland, a four-qubit ($16\times16$) problem reached a relative residual below $10^{-7}$ within eight to ten corrections in all 20 runs of a pre-registered experiment, whereas a single solve with a comparable number of shots stopped at 0.33-0.59. In a six-qubit ($64\times64$) pilot, all three runs reached the same tolerance in 13 corrections. On the IonQ Forte processor, a manufactured-solution benchmark reached $5.6\times10^{-8}$ in nine corrections, with a pressure error of $4.9\times10^{-8}$. Finally, we carried out the variational training itself on ibm_cleveland, so that every sampled value of the cost function was obtained from hardware measurements. The residual fell to $5.6\times10^{-4}$ in seven corrections, below the pre-specified tolerance of $10^{-3}$, at the rate predicted by a noise model and with 24 min of QPU time.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum decoding succeeds when a correction matches the logical class of the error, whereas ordered-statistics decoding (OSD) searches a finite list of physical corrections. We connect these spaces through a rank-$k$ logical-signature map $M_{\rm log}:\mathbb F_2^q\to\mathbb F_2^k$. For fixed, error-independent orderings it yields the exact logical-signature distribution, the OSD-0 logical error rate, and the logical-class coverage of any fixed list. For minimum-cost list decoding we prove $P_L=P_{\rm miss}+P_{\rm sel}$, separating absent-class from wrong-selection failures. On the $[\![144,12,12]\!]$ gross code, 672 coverage-aware candidates outperform 899 combination-sweep candidates (paired McNemar $p=0.013$), while two lists with identical logical coverage give $P_L=11.30\%$ and $3.78\%$; the signature-based coverage compression persists across 30 random column orderings. Logical coverage and within-class representative selection are therefore complementary resources that candidate count alone does not capture. The framework guides OSD list construction and compression by identifying coverage requirements and distinguishing coverage losses from failures of representative selection.

Abstract: Understanding the generation and activation of ergotropy, the maximum work extractable by cyclic unitary operations, is central to quantum battery research. Positive-temperature thermal states are passive, whereas nonequilibrium environments can sustain nonpassive steady states with nonzero ergotropy. In contrast, detuned qubits can exhibit incoherent ergotropy. Comparing NESSs obtained with and without full secularization reveals quantitative and qualitative differences in ergotropy that reflect changes in both its coherent and incoherent contributions. The results clarify the mechanisms of NESS ergotropy activation and demonstrate the importance of retaining steady-state coherence beyond the full secular approximation when characterizing stationary work resources.

Abstract: Exchange-based two-qubit gates in semiconductor spin qubits are sensitive to low-frequency detuning noise and control errors. We find that CCD retains strong protection against quasistatic common-mode detuning errors while substantially improving robustness against Rabi-frequency errors and qubit-to-qubit Rabi-frequency mismatch compared with conventional continuous dressing. By contrast, the gate fidelity is impacted by differential detuning errors. By modeling partially correlated quasistatic detuning noise, we further show that the noise-averaged gate fidelity depends strongly on the spatial correlation of the qubit-frequency fluctuations, with positive correlations enhancing CCD protection by suppressing the differential-noise component. Abstract: Collective coupling to the Minkowski vacuum allows uniformly accelerated quantum detectors to exhibit a strong quantum Mpemba effect, in which a state initially farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than a closer one. We establish the existence of this effect for every fixed finite number of detectors $N\geq 2$ at sufficiently small transverse separation within the pointlike secular master equation. The mechanism originates from two reflection sectors whose populations are conserved by the leading collective dynamics. Weak intersector transfer at finite separation generates an isolated, parametrically slow relaxation mode. A simple family of initial states permits its leading-order cancellation by matching the equilibrium sector populations, while a corrected preparation eliminates the slow-mode amplitude exactly. We construct a reference state that establishes a genuine reversal of trace-distance ordering. Numerical calculations and local-rate-preserving controls identify the collective origin of the effect, while spatially smeared detector calculations support its persistence within the second-order secular framework. The same mechanism extends to detectors coupled to a common thermal field, with analytically distinct slow relaxation rates. Thus, strong Mpemba relaxation is not exclusive to acceleration but emerges from a more general interplay between collective dissipation, reflection-sector conservation, and weak symmetry-breaking transfer.

Abstract: Mixed-state quantum geometry plays a fundamental role in quantum metrology, multiparameter estimation, and the characterization of quantum many-body systems. Yet, unlike its pure-state counterpart, accessing mixed-state geometry requires solving an ill-conditioned Lyapunov equation for the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD), posing a major challenge for resource-efficient quantum computation. Here we address the challenge and develop a novel quantum algorithmic framework for efficiently accessing the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) and quantum geometric tensor (QGT) of mixed states. Our key insight is to uncover a previously unexplored connection between the positive semidefinite structure of density matrices and exponentially convergent Gauss-Laguerre quadrature, enabling an efficient integral-based construction of the SLD. A rigorous complexity analysis further establishes the resource efficiency of our algorithm, demonstrating logarithmic precision dependence and double-logarithmic ancillary-qubit overhead at fixed conditioning. Abstract: Reliable quantum computation requires methods for verifying quantum circuits. We prove that acyclic LSTAs have NP-complete nonemptiness and $Π_2^P$-complete language inclusion, and that general language inclusion is EXPSPACE-complete. We further prove inherent quadratic representation growth under individual quantum gates in a fixed qubit order, and give an adjacent-qubit reordering construction that preserves level synchronization. These results clarify the scope of applicability of the automata-theoretic toolbox for quantum circuit verification.

A physics-inspired Generator-A dataset with 5 million balanced records was constructed using explicit mathematical generation models for all four features. A transparent threshold baseline, logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on Generator-A. The full-feature learning models achieved macro F1-scores from 0.8211 to 0.8243 and outperformed the transparent two-feature comparator. Feature diagnostics showed that QBER contributed most, followed by latency in milliseconds and photon count, while no single feature reproduced the full feature set performance. Robustness was assessed on a separately generated Generator-B dataset of 5 million records that uses the same governing equations under shifted simulated operating parameters. Without retraining or tuning, their macro F1-scores decreased to values from 0.5645 to 0.5669. These findings show that QF-LOG can support interpretable forensic classification in a controlled synthetic setting, but stable internal evaluation does not establish reliability under changed simulated QKD operating conditions. The framework provides a documented basis for future evaluation across broader simulated settings and hardware-generated QKD logs.

Abstract: Nonrelativistic quantum chemistry is based on the Schrödinger equation in continuous, unbounded space. Computational approaches on classical and quantum computers often truncate the spatial domain and introduce a finite-dimensional basis, reducing the continuum problem to a finite-dimensional one. There is therefore a fundamental question: to what extent does this approximation capture the original continuum problem? In this work, we develop a general Sobolev-Galerkin framework for estimating spatial discretization errors in quantum simulation with unbounded Hamiltonians. We apply this framework to many-body Coulomb systems and establish both periodization and Galerkin error estimates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies. For dynamics, the finite-basis projection error of the exact wave function does not capture the full basis-discretization error: an additional dynamical projection error enters our bound at a different rate, and we introduce an inner-product argument to control this contribution. Interestingly, the spatial convergence rates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies differ quantitatively. For ground-state energies, we establish a sharp $M^{-1}$ convergence rate at fixed cell size, where $M$ is the number of one-particle basis functions. We also show that a uniform $M^{-1}$ convergence rate is impossible for the corresponding real-time wave-function error with general $H^2$ initial data. The analysis relies on many-body Sobolev estimates with explicit polynomial dependence on system size. Combining these estimates with state-of-the-art quantum algorithms, we obtain, to our knowledge, the first rigorous end-to-end complexity bounds for quantum simulation of molecular and electronic dynamics with all spatial errors explicitly controlled. The number of basis functions is determined directly from the continuum problem, without regularization.

Abstract: Randomness certification aims to guarantee that the output of an untrusted quantum device contains genuine randomness that remains unpredictable even given quantum side information held by an adversary. For a classical verifier interacting with a single quantum device, such a guarantee cannot rely on the physical separation underlying standard Bell tests and must instead follow from computational restrictions on the prover. Building on the framework of Computational Bell Inequalities, we distinguish classical and quantum virtual-input hiding, showing that the former suffices for proofs of quantumness, whereas the latter enables randomness certification against quantum side information.

We show that statistical hiding together with ordinary computational binding against quantum polynomial-time senders suffices, without requiring stronger quantum binding notions such as sum-binding or collapse-binding. This yields a randomness-certification protocol from post-quantum statistically hiding bit commitments. In particular, existing constructions of such commitments from post-quantum one-way permutations imply that post-quantum one-way permutations suffice for randomness certification.
  We further instantiate our construction using quantum-secure trapdoor claw-free functions, obtaining an efficient classical verifier, and in the quantum random oracle model, obtaining randomness certification without additional computational assumptions but with an inefficient verifier. These instantiations separate the cryptographic properties sufficient for randomness certification from the additional structure required for efficient verification within our commitment-based framework.

Abstract: Laser amplification kicks off with just a few photons in the cavity, a regime where standard additive Langevin noise cannot capture the real fluctuations. To understand how this amplification begins, we map out a single-mode photon-carrier model that links macroscopic lasers directly into nanolasers. Stationary rate equations reveal that stimulated emission takes over across a tight pump window. In nanolasers, however, spontaneous processes coexist across a much broader range. Because steady-state averages miss the individual events that lead to the macroscopic photon number, we frame the stochastic dynamics around the actual discrete transitions, deriving a state-dependent diffusion approximation. The destruction of an excited carrier concurrent with the generation of a photon creates a negative photon-carrier cross-correlation. This unavoidably leads to a correlated, multiplicative Gaussian-noise framework. We track the resulting discrete dynamics with an established, event-based Laser Markov Chain (LMC) which stochastically samples pump, relaxation, emission, and cavity-loss events keeping all physical quantities as non-negative integers. Comparing its predictions to those of our multiplicative model proves that the latter reproduces excellently well the statistical behavior of photons and carrier, as long as a continuous diffusion description holds. Once the emission drops into the few-photon regime, however, the discrete model becomes indispensable. On the other hand, at large photon counts relative fluctuations shrink to a level where the state-dependence of the diffusion matrix can be ignored, to leading order, thus returning to the classic Langevin description of the system's stochastic dynamics.

Abstract: We study whether a quantum verifier can find a hidden quantum state by exchanging classical messages with an unbounded prover who knows the state. The verifier has access to a reflection oracle about the target. For a Haar-random $n$-qubit target, we prove that achieving constant average fidelity with the target requires $(q+1)^2(B+1)=Ω(2^n)$, where $q$ is the number of verifier queries and $B$ is the total prover-to-verifier communication. The bound allows arbitrary classical interaction and unrestricted computation between queries. Our proof extends the lower bound of Aaronson and Kuperberg from a single classical proof to an arbitrary classical interaction.
  As a result, we obtain a quantum-oracle separation of UniqueQMA from QCIP with polynomially many classical messages. In particular, we give a unitary oracle $\mathcal{U}$ such that there exists a language $L\in\mathrm{UniqueQMA}^{\mathcal{U}}$ but $L\notin\mathrm{QCIP}^{\mathcal{U}}$.
  We further give an oracle construction for a notion known as QMA-QCIP[2] gap problem. This gives average case hardness for a QMA language under classical access to a QCIP[2] decision oracle and is used in establishing negative results on witness encryption and quantum indistinguishability obfuscation.

Abstract: Complex amplitudes can be simulated over real Hilbert spaces by adjoining a two-dimensional real/imaginary degree of freedom. In a multipartite program, however, one globally shared phase tag is not a locality-preserving program transformation: every party would have to act on the same auxiliary system. For $n$ parties we give explicit phase-reference codewords $|0\rangle_{P_n}$ and $|1\rangle_{P_n}$ on $n$ local tags and prove that the same real operator $J=XZ$ on any single tag realizes the logical action of multiplication by $i$. If a complex party-local gate is $A=A_R+iA_I$, its real translation is \[
  \widehat A_j=A_R\otimes I+A_I\otimes J_j, \] which acts only on party $j$'s data and local phase tag. We prove an intertwining theorem for arbitrary finite party-local pure-state circuits, exact preservation of computational-basis measurement probabilities, preservation of the run-time interaction graph, and one-rebit-per-party overhead. For the canonical three-party GHZ program, the translation lies entirely inside the ordinary black-and-white misty gate fragment: controlled-$J$ replaces the complex phase operation, while the invariant $J_jJ_k|0\rangle_P=-|0\rangle_P$ transfers the required branch sign. This yields a short correctness proof for all four promised GHZ inputs and explains the earlier 64-path cancellation calculation at a single logical phase-code boundary. We distinguish this program realification problem from the weaker task of finding any real strategy with the same correlations, and state precisely which parts are known real-Hilbert-space simulation and which parts belong to the misty-language compilation layer developed here.

Abstract: The quantum phase of a multiparticle system has remained a long-standing mystery in quantum physics. The absence of a phase operator that faithfully represents this physical quantity has prevented the formulation of a meaningful uncertainty relation with particle number. Here, we resolve this problem by measuring, for the first time, the quantum phase of multiparticle systems containing up to fifteen particles. We achieve this through a new quantum state tomography scheme that combines homodyne interferometry with photon-number-resolving detection. This approach enables us to reconstruct complementary photon-number and phase representations and establish the corresponding number-phase uncertainty relation. Our measurements further provide access to the mutually unbiased basis of a multiparticle system, a fundamental resource for multiparticle quantum information processing. Furthermore, we report the spatially resolved characterization of the quantum phase. These results provide an operational framework for measuring quantum phase in multiparticle systems, with implications for fundamental quantum physics and scalable multiparticle quantum technologies.

Abstract: The formulation of quantum measurements compatible with relativistic causality is obstructed by the tension between the locality of observable algebras and the dynamical state transformations induced by measurement processes. Measurement processes are described by localized completely positive maps and organized into operational measurement protocols, finite non-adaptive sequences of local instruments distributed across the causal cone induced by the automaton local dynamics. Each protocol induces an effective measurement on the initial time slice, whose support is constrained by the causal structure generated by the automaton. Non-local measurement effects therefore arise through causal propagation and composition of local operations, rather than from primitive non-local interventions or instantaneous collapse prescriptions. We establish the corresponding spatial support bounds and characterize the resulting measurement architecture. As an explicit example, we construct a two-step protocol for a one-dimensional nearest-neighbour qubit automaton which, for suitable automaton parameters, realizes a Bell-basis projective measurement on two neighbouring sites after coarse-graining of the local outcomes. Away from this point, the same protocol generates a continuous family of deformed Bell measurements whose entanglement and stabilizer Rényi non-classicality can be quantified explicitly. The framework thus provides a causally consistent operator-algebraic mechanism for synthesizing spatially extended quantum measurements from strictly local operations.

This approach could help make coherent processing of molecular geometry ensembles a useful primitive for fault-tolerant quantum computing.

The proposed dual-satellite architecture mitigates this limitation and significantly extends the achievable communication distance, representing a key step toward scalable quantum internet infrastructures.

Its positive class-level differences are concentrated in CEP, RRC, and ROT, while the full-budget LightGBM reference scores higher on both metrics. All circuits are simulated classically. The comparisons retain existing validation-selected checkpoints and use the same test objects, with features, predictions, and recomputation code publicly available. Abstract: Quantum communication networks promise information-theoretic security but this security collapses if intermediate relay nodes are compromised, a risk that grows sharply when compromised nodes cannot be identified. We address this twofold: first, we show that multi-path routing provides quantifiable protection against unidentified compromised nodes. Second, we provide a comprehensive statistical and graph-theoretic analysis of network and node security under compromise, covering full and partial node compromise, optimal placement of additional edges via an iterative method, and comparative hacking-probability analysis across network topologies. Together, these results identify the topology and multi-path routing strategy that most improves security for a given network. We apply this framework to quantum key distribution across multiple network topologies, demonstrating a scalable route to secure, reliable quantum communication under realistic, unidentified adversarial threats, without relying on quantum memory.

Abstract: We investigate open-system charging protocols for Rabi and Dicke quantum batteries under direct incoherent pumping. The charging dynamics follows a Lindblad master equation, while effective non-Hermitian generators describe the conditioned no-jump mechanism. For a single Rabi battery, this pumping suppresses coherent energy backflow, broadens the high-energy charging window, and improves robustness against both timing errors and detuning. At the selected Rabi operating points, pumping also increases ergotropy and the extractable fraction of the stored energy. For finite Dicke batteries, direct incoherent pumping likewise stabilizes the charging dynamics. Across the finite-size scan, the strongest pump considered improves the peak stored energy and ergotropy per cell and near-peak timing robustness relative to the Hermitian reference. The maximum average charging power also increases, although this metric includes pump-assisted jump contributions. Equal-input comparisons show that the relative performance of common-cavity Dicke and independent Rabi batteries varies with the pump-input bound. Taken together, these results show that direct incoherent pumping provides a route to stabilized charging in Rabi and finite Dicke quantum batteries.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的哈密顿重整化群建立了不同尺度下物理系统有效描述之间的关系。受此启发,作者希望构建一个量子控制的重整化群框架,解决量子控制中的核心问题:如何系统性地将理论上设计的控制哈密顿量(可能包含实验上难以实现的复杂相互作用项)转化为仅使用实验可及控制项的等效协议,同时保持所需的量子演化目标。这为非平衡量子动力学中看似不同的问题(如Floquet工程和快速绝热捷径)提供统一的组织原则。

Conclusion: 该量子控制重整化群框架揭示了哈密顿工程和幺正量子控制的统一视角,可作为理解看似不同的非平衡量子动力学现象背后的组织原则。通过将重整化群思想引入量子控制领域,该方法提供了一个系统化工具,能够在实验约束下设计可实现的量子控制协议,在Floquet工程、反绝热快速驱动等多个量子控制问题中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: The Renormalization Group (RG) establishes relations between effective descriptions of physical systems at different scales; in Hamiltonian RG, these relations induce a flow in the space of Hamiltonians. In direct correspondence, we design an RG framework for quantum control by constructing a flow in the space of time-dependent Hamiltonians. Abstract: The Parity Architecture was originally introduced in the context of quantum optimization algorithms to overcome hardware connectivity constraints. Abstract: Utility-scale quantum technologies will not only require a well-performing quantum platform, but also classical control hardware that is scalable, efficient, and meets the design requirements set by the quantum system of interest. Abstract: We examined the energy-momentum relationship of the valence band of Cu$_2$O, which has gained interest as an emerging solid host for giant Rydberg states.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了四量子比特双曲二次曲面语境度猜想，证明了其精确值为315，并完整刻画了所有最优非语境赋值的结构。手工证明的下界方法简洁优雅，基于doily和Peres-Mermin网格的组合结构。最优解的局部刻画条件不仅给出了有效的SAT编码，还揭示了问题的深层几何结构。形式化验证和证明认证确保了结果的可靠性。该方法成功推广到更高维情况，为研究量子语境性的组合结构提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: The contextuality degree of a set of Pauli contexts is the least number of contexts that every noncontextual $\pm1$ assignment violates. For the four-qubit hyperbolic quadric $\mathcal{Q}^{+}(7,2)$, with $135$ points and $1575$ lines as contexts, Muller et al. conjectured from heuristic searches that the degree is $315$ and that the violated contexts of every optimal assignment form a dual polar space $DW(5,2)$. We prove both conjectures. The lower bound has a short proof by hand: every doily ($GQ(2,2)$) of the quadric contains ten Peres-Mermin grids covering each of its lines four times, so every assignment violates at least three of its fifteen lines, and averaging over the doilies gives $1575/5=315$. The equality case is local: an assignment is optimal exactly when it violates one ruling of one grid in every doily. Using this as a SAT encoding, we show that there are exactly $240$ optimal violated sets, one orbit of $\mathrm{O}^+(8,2)$, each isomorphic to $DW(5,2)$. The same argument gives the first lower bounds $\lceil L/5\rceil$ for all quadrics with $L$ lines in $N\ge4$ qubits. The degree theorem is kernel-checked in Lean 4, and the completeness of the enumeration is certified by a DRAT proof checked by the verified checker cake_lpr.

</details>

Abstract: Fault-tolerant (FT) quantum computation offers a pathway to quantum advantage, but its realization is hindered by its significant overhead. The partially FT "STAR" architecture reduces the cost of universal computation at the price of relaxing FT requirements for non-Clifford operations, increasing exposure to hardware noise. Therefore, maximizing its performance and applicability demands careful co-design with the underlying hardware. In this work we investigate the co-design of the STAR architecture with neutral-atom platforms, connecting the underlying atomic physics to logical error modeling and application-level performance. At the physical level, we identify a favorable noise bias in $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ Rydberg gates, and show how it can be enhanced through error-selective pulse optimization. At the logical level, we identify an intrinsic error suppression mechanism, yielding vanishing logical coherent noise in leading order. Detailed circuit-level simulations of surface code implementations show more than an order of magnitude reduction in logical error relative to previous estimates. As a concrete target application, we focus on the Fermi-Hubbard model with periodic boundaries, for which we design a simulation circuit particularly suited to early implementations. We quantify the resource tradeoff of partially and fully FT approaches to gate synthesis, with the former showing approximately an order of magnitude reduction in state-injection depth, and an even larger reduction in Clifford resources. Comparing with classical and fully FT approaches, we identify regimes where partial fault tolerance provides both lower overheads and lower error rates. In our estimates, this is accessible with moderate error rates $p\sim 10^{-3}$ and a few thousand qubits, underlining that for these types of problems, partial FT approaches provide a compelling pathway to early quantum advantage.

</details>


### [75] [Majorization for superpositions of nonorthogonal states and the existence of golden states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26483)

TL;DR: 本文研究了非正交参考态叠加资源理论中黄金态的存在性及其Löwdin基结构，证明了鲁棒性和几何度量由等重叠态最大化，并建立了Gram控制作为纯态转换的充要判据，揭示了三参考态情形下黄金态存在的充要条件依赖于重叠模和Bargmann相位的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非正交线性独立参考态的叠加资源理论框架中，黄金态（golden states）仅在特定情况下已知（两个参考态或特殊重叠矩阵），且均在Löwdin基中具有最大相干性。然而，决定黄金态存在性的根本因素及其Löwdin基结构的普适规律尚不清楚，需要系统阐明不同自由操作下黄金态的刻画条件和存在性判据。

Method: 1) 分析叠加鲁棒性和几何度量，证明其由与所有参考态等重叠的态最大化，最大值由对偶基重叠的经典XY模型基态能量决定；2) 对最大无叠加自由操作，证明等重叠态是唯一的黄金态；3) 对Kraus型自由操作，提出并证明Gram控制作为非零系数纯态转换的充要判据（正交参考态时退化为控制关系）；4) 针对三参考态情形，推导黄金态存在的充要条件，建立重叠模s、Bargmann相位Φ与存在性的定量关系s≥2cos[(π+|Φ|)/3]；5) 构造基于等重叠态的Löwdin基完备叠加见证族。

The robustness and the geometric measure of superposition are maximized by the same states, which overlap equally with every reference state; their maximal values are set by the ground-state energy of a classical $XY$ model on the overlaps of the dual basis. For every overlap matrix, these states, and only these, are golden under the maximal superposition-free operations, but they need not be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. For the Kraus-type free operations, we prove a necessary and sufficient criterion, Gram majorization, for converting pure states into targets with nonzero coefficients; it reduces to majorization for orthonormal reference states. Abstract: An important aspect of many linear optical quantum computing architectures is probabilistically generating small dual-rail qubit entangled states, or seed states. While many schemes to achieve this have been discovered, attention has been primarily focused on minimizing input resources and maximizing success probability rather than establishing a detailed understanding of linear-optical error performance. In this work, we develop a framework for resolving sensitivities to physically distinct errors in linear optical state generation, allowing us to identify precise ways in which these errors affect the properties of the output state. We use this framework to identify seed state generators in which destructive many-photon interference prevents detector loss and detector noise from causing a falsely successful heralding event. We show that this effect suppresses errors, for example cutting the effective loss on the output of particular Bell state generators in half relative to a standard scheme. We also present a new Bell state generator in which both detector loss and detector noise do not affect the quality of the output Bell state to first order, and a 4GHZ generator with >75% higher success probability than a standard circuit. In addition we provide some useful identities, bounds and invariants for studying linear optical seed state generation.

Abstract: The primary pathway to the investigation of exceptional points (EPs) in open quantum systems has been the use of Markovian master equations. However, recently it has been realised that they can arise in settings that show non-Markovian behaviour. The main approach in this direction has been the use of pseudomodes, which allow the influence of a structured environment to be replaced by new damped auxiliary modes. In this work, we use a Heisenberg-equation approach which relies on the reaction-coordinate formalism to obtain exact analytical dynamics. First, we show how our method considerably simplifies the analysis compared to the use of master equations. Second, we utilise it to discuss non-Markovian revivals in systems with second and higher-order EPs, showing that the notion that EPs demarcate the onset of revivals is not generally true. We then utilise our results to discuss the dynamical effects of higher-order non-Markovian EPs, deriving bounds on the number of revivals and investigating enhanced asymptotic relaxation near higher-order EPs. Finally, we investigate non-Markovian EPs with engineered reservoirs and show how they can be utilised to control the rate of irreversible information loss.

Abstract: Quantum annealing generally samples degenerate ground states with nonuniform probabilities. This unfair sampling depends on how the driver Hamiltonian couples these states near the end of annealing. For combinatorial optimization problems with a cardinality constraint, the penalty method and constrained quantum annealing (CQA) differ in these couplings. The transverse-field driver of the penalty method does not directly couple feasible ground states, whereas the fully connected XY driver of CQA couples pairs of them related by a single spin exchange. We investigate how this difference affects ground-state sampling, using closed-system simulations of random graph bipartitioning instances together with degenerate perturbation theory. For the instances studied, the conditional ground-state distribution of penalty-based quantum annealing approaches that of CQA at sufficiently large penalties and long annealing times. This convergence can be explained by the effective Hamiltonian of penalty-based quantum annealing, in which virtual single-spin flips to infeasible states generate a second-order interaction proportional to the CQA driver projected onto the feasible ground-state manifold, which is the leading term of the effective Hamiltonian of CQA. In the numerical simulations, the Jensen--Shannon divergence between the conditional ground-state distributions of penalty-based quantum annealing and CQA decreases approximately as the inverse square of the penalty-to-objective energy ratio, consistent with the perturbative analysis. This correspondence between the effective Hamiltonians extends to equal-weight fixed-magnetization constraints under the same perturbative conditions, providing a common mechanism for sampling bias in the two implementations.

Abstract: Accurate modelling of chemical reaction rates requires precise correlated energy differences. Quantum computing offers a potential route to their determination, but extracting energies from quantum-prepared states can demand prohibitive sampling. We demonstrate measurement-efficient correlated energy estimation using an electronically challenging H$_2$DBB--Ru model derived from a Ru-functionalized hafnium metal-organic framework relevant to radiodynamic and photodynamic therapies. The mixed estimator combines a quantum-prepared target with sparse multiconfiguration references and recovers ground-state corrections using magnitude-only compute-uncompute measurements. Analytical variance estimates predict 1.6 mHa precision with a few hundred shots, about 100 times fewer than the single-determinant formulation and three orders of magnitude fewer than optimized Hamiltonian-term sampling. With approximate targets, it reaches comparable accuracy using simpler state preparation than direct expectation-value estimation. Emulator and trapped-ion experiments on Quantinuum Helios validate the approach up to (10,10), including a 50-qubit circuit combining AI-assisted state preparation with error detection, providing a measurement-efficient route to correlated energy differences for challenging chemical problems.

Abstract: We determine the exact quantum communication and entanglement-distribution capacities of three fundamental bosonic channels, namely thermal loss, thermal amplification and additive Gaussian noise, assisted by operations that completely preserve positive partial transpose (PPT). These operations are more powerful than local operations and classical communication (LOCC), but cannot generate distillable entanglement on their own. For thermal loss, the unconstrained capacity coincides with the Pirandola-Laurenza-Ottaviani-Banchi (PLOB) bound throughout the non-entanglement-breaking regime. The corresponding upper bounds are also attained for thermal amplification and additive noise. We extend these results to energy-constrained communication, establishing exact capacities for all three channels at every prescribed mean input energy. Our results solve a class of bosonic capacity problems under PPT assistance and establish exact benchmarks for determining whether the same communication rates can be achieved via LOCC alone.

Abstract: We benchmark the Iterative Redecode Scheme (IRS) on fast transversal Clifford circuits, achieving high-accuracy correlated decoding using local memory-style detectors and decoders. On a deep random Clifford benchmark, IRS yields approximately $4\times$ lower logical failure rates than the ghost protocol at $d=11$ with matched buffers, and $15\times$ lower rates than logical observable matching (LOM). In three-block GHZ preparation, IRS achieves $2\times$ lower logical error rates than reliable logical Pauli-product decoding with matched initialisation, improving to $25\times$ at $d=19$ with $\lceil d/2\rceil$ full initialisation rounds. We place IRS, which builds on Wan et al.'s scheme, within a broader propagation-assisted local (PAL) framework, where local decoders exchange messages rather than decoding a global hypergraph. IRS alternates local minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM) on memory-equivalent detector error models (DEMs) with Pauli-frame propagation and detector updates across transversal CNOT layers, seeking local error estimates consistent with the global detector record. Prior-informed DEMs account for probabilities of propagated errors missing from the memory-equivalent model, improving initial decoding and reducing logical error rates. Zero-weight physical Pauli mechanisms enable inference and propagation of initially indeterminate stabilizer outcomes. Error rates saturate after a few iterations even for deep, adversarial alternating-tCNOT circuits, allowing iteration caps with negligible loss of accuracy. We find syndrome-extraction buffers of order $\lfloor d/4\rfloor$ sufficient for distance-dependent error suppression in the demanding regimes studied. The approach extends to other local decoders and CSS codes; for repeated logical $S$ gates, IRS achieves approximately $4$ to $10\times$ lower logical error rates than LOM, depending on initialisation.

</details>

Result: 正则化重加权规则成功恢复了距离为d的表面码的标称距离缩放特性,使解码器能够正确纠正尺寸为(d-1)/2的错误配置。实验结果显示,改进后的方法在电路级噪声下展现出明确的关联解码优势,错误抑制性能得到显著提升,特别是在阈值以下的区域。

Conclusion: 标准关联解码的重加权方法存在系统性缺陷,会导致量子纠错码无法达到其理论纠错距离。通过引入正则化的重加权规则,可以修正这一问题,恢复码的标称距离缩放性能,并确保关联解码方法能够可靠地提供性能优势。这一发现对于实现高保真度的量子计算系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Correlated decoding improves matching and clustering surface-code decoders by reweighting one decoding subgraph conditioned on the other's correction. We show that standard reweighting can cause correlated decoders to fail on size-$(d-1)/2$ fault configurations that a distance-$d$ code should correct, reducing error suppression deep below threshold. Under circuit-level noise, a single first-pass edge can discount several correlated edges, even though their joint occurrence requires additional independent faults, so the decoder favors an over-discounted correction. Regularizing the reweighting rule restores the nominal distance scaling and a clear correlated-decoding advantage.

</details>

Conclusion: 研究表明在相互作用拓扑系统中构造有效的局域反绝热项需要考虑微观物理机制。通过根据相互作用能量代价分解电流并采用基态加权变分作用量,可以构造出在短周期时间内保持量子化泵浦的局域近似哈密顿量。这一方法克服了传统变分框架在强关联拓扑系统中的局限性,为多体拓扑相的快速绝热操控提供了新思路,具有良好的系统尺寸标度性质。

Abstract: Counterdiabatic (CD) driving suppresses the excitations of fast protocols by adding an auxiliary Hamiltonian, but in many-body and topological systems the exact CD term is generally non-local and multi-body. We study this obstruction for Thouless pumping in the Rice-Mele model, first without and then with interactions. For noninteracting fermions, the exact CD term of the pump is non-local, but the variational framework of Sels and Polkovnikov yields accurate nearest-neighbor approximations. For the spinful Rice-Mele chain with onsite and nearest-neighbor repulsion, in the regime where the competition of the two interactions stabilizes quantized pumping, this approach fails: the infinite-temperature variational action only penalizes the CD amplitudes, and the resulting two-parameter nearest-neighbor term does not prevent the pump from breaking down when the cycle time is reduced from $50/t$ to $2/t$. We trace the failure to the structure of the exact many-body gauge potential, whose amplitude for each hop depends on the interaction energy that the hop costs. Resolving the nearest-neighbor currents by this energy, and fixing the coefficients with a ground-state-weighted action, gives a local ansatz with 14 coefficients that keeps the interacting pump quantized for cycle times $T=2/t$ and $T=1/t$, with an accuracy per site that does not degrade between rings of 8 and 12 sites.

</details>

Abstract: Machine learning is widely regarded as a promising application in which quantum computing could offer a computational advantage. However, general quantum machine learning models with provable guarantees on performance, trainability, and efficient inference remain limited. In this work, we introduce quantum Hamiltonian embeddings, a supervised learning framework in which a classical training dataset is encoded into a single effective, parameterized Hamiltonian. Training optimizes the parameters of this data-dependent Hamiltonian, whose approximate ground state defines the classification rule. Once the ground state has been approximated using a quantum algorithm, inference can be performed either classically using a classical representation of the learned state, or with a quantum representation of the state. We establish efficient methods for gradient estimation and prove favorable generalization guarantees. Furthermore, under standard cryptographic assumptions, we show that Hamiltonian embeddings can realize hypothesis functions beyond the reach of efficient classical algorithms. We then demonstrate the framework at scale on IBM Heron processors using a standard benchmark dataset. Together, these results establish Hamiltonian embeddings as a new framework for quantum machine learning, combining theoretical guarantees with a path for experimental investigations at scale on quantum hardware.

</details>

*Xiajie Huang,Cristina Álvarez Yuste,Nikita Guseynov,Nana Liu,Javier González-Conde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于有限时间区间最优控制的量子算法,特别针对连续时间线性二次调节器(LQR)问题。通过将问题重构为多重打靶线性系统并利用量子线性系统求解器,实现了对系统规模和控制时长的多项式对数级复杂度,避免了求解非线性Riccati方程的高昂成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典求解器在处理大规模系统和长时间控制的线性二次调节器(LQR)问题时,需要求解非线性矩阵Riccati方程,计算成本高昂。现有方法在系统规模和控制时长增加时面临计算效率瓶颈,因此需要开发更高效的求解方法来处理有限时间区间的最优控制问题。

Method: 将LQR问题重构为基于状态-协态最优性方程的多重打靶线性系统,而非直接求解Riccati方程。在可稳定性和可检测性假设下,证明了系统条件数界与时间区间无关。通过将初始和终止条件整合到单一线性系统中,利用量子线性系统子程序高效求解。算法使用稀疏矩阵块编码、初始状态制备预言机和固定数量的控制输入,直接返回最优代价和特定时刻最优控制输入的经典估计值,无需构造Riccati解算子。

Result: 在稀疏性、相关范数和稳定性参数有界的条件下,量子算法的查询和门复杂度相对于系统规模和控制时长呈多项式对数级缩放;对目标误差的依赖性为逆线性(除对数因子外)。对于具有两个端点控制的阻尼质量-弹簧链系统族,建立了在系统规模和时长上一致的所需界。数值实验验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了针对有限时间区间LQR问题的量子算法,通过多重打靶重构和量子线性求解器实现了显著的计算复杂度优势。证明了关键的条件数界与时间区间无关的性质,算法复杂度在系统规模和控制时长上实现了多项式对数级缩放。该方法为大规模、长时间最优控制问题提供了量子加速的可行途径,并在具体物理系统中得到了验证。

Abstract: We develop quantum algorithms for optimal control over a prescribed finite time interval (finite horizon), focusing on continuous-time linear-quadratic regulation (LQR). This foundational control problem often requires solving a nonlinear matrix Riccati equation, which can be costly for large systems and long control durations on conventional classical solvers. We instead work directly with the state-costate optimality equations, reformulating the LQR problem as a multiple-shooting linear system. Importantly, we prove a condition-number bound for the system that is independent of the horizon, under stabilizability and detectability assumptions. By incorporating the initial and final conditions into a single linear system, we can solve the subsequent linear system more efficiently with a quantum linear systems subroutine. For a given initial state, our quantum algorithms return classical estimates of the optimal cost and the optimal control inputs at a selected time, without constructing a Riccati solution operator. Given sparse-matrix block-encodings, an initial-state preparation oracle and a fixed number of control inputs, the query and gate complexities scale polylogarithmically with system size and control duration, provided that sparsity, relevant norms, and stability parameters are bounded. The dependence on the target error is inverse-linear up to logarithmic factors. We establish the required bounds uniformly in system size and duration for a family of damped mass-spring chains with two endpoint controls. Numerical experiments support the analysis.

</details>

Result: 证明了EFT膜的标度向量位于EFT弦生成的格子中，与之前粒子塔的发现一致，并在F理论和Type IIA紧化中确定了具体的相对标度关系。在F理论中排除了两个候选加速轨迹，因为这些情况下EFT弦会变得比哈勃尺度更轻，导致四维有效场论失效。这一结果封闭了arXiv:2209.11821中发现的理论漏洞。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了四维N=1理论中粒子塔、EFT弦和EFT膜之间存在系统的标度层级结构关系。虽然自下而上的分析没有给出通量紧化中渐近加速膨胀的普遍性阻碍，但通过EFT弦质量约束成功排除了特定的加速轨迹。量子阻碍效应可能通过改变Kähler势的渐近形式提供具体的解决方案，为理解弦论紧化中的宇宙学问题提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study the interplay among towers of particles, EFT strings, and EFT membranes near perturbative infinite-distance limits of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories. We show that the scaling vectors of EFT membranes lie in the lattice generated by those of the EFT strings, as previously found for particle towers, and determine their specific relative scaling in F-theory and Type IIA Calabi-Yau compactifications. We then leverage these objects to assess the viability of asymptotic accelerated expansion in flux compactifications. While our bottom-up analysis does not yield a general obstruction, we rule out two candidate accelerating trajectories in F-theory because an EFT string becomes lighter than the Hubble scale, invalidating the four-dimensional EFT. This closes the loophole found in arXiv:2209.11821. We also study how recent results on quantum obstructions to $\mathcal{N}=1$ infinite-distance limits may provide a concrete resolution by changing the asymptotic form of the Kähler potential.

</details>

Abstract: We calculate the total loss of angular momentum for a gravitational scattering of two non-spinning point-like objects at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$. Our methodology combines use of both amplitudes and worldline-based methods. We derive the integrand from two copies of the gravitational waveform with cuts on massive lines arising naturally from the worldline. We then glue these two copies and evaluate the resulting phase-space integrals using reverse unitarity, in the amplitudes spirit. We obtain separately the radiated angular momentum and the static contribution that, at this order, is entirely determined by the initial shear and the linear memory. Combining these two terms, we derive relativistic expressions for the angular momentum loss and for the infrared-finite component of the mass dipole loss, which in the low-velocity limit match results available in the literature. We also use this result to derive the conservative deflection angle at $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ and first self-force order.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了全局德西特(dS)背景下微扰量子引力的群平均内积的正定性问题。证明了在特定粒子权重条件下(Re ∑Δᵢ > d-1)该内积为正,但发现某些小权重态可能具有负范数,这对德西特量子引力的微扰理论具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微扰量子引力在全局德西特背景下存在约束条件,要求所有由连续德西特等距变换定义的荷量在线性化态中必须为零。标准Fock空间中唯一满足这些约束的可归一化态是真空态,导致允许态的缺失。Higuchi提出使用群平均内积来解决这一问题,但该内积的正定性一直是悬而未决的开放问题,需要被严格证明以确保理论的一致性。

Method: 研究采用群平均内积方法处理德西特空间中的量子态。针对d>2维情况,分析了标准全局dS模式中N粒子态(包括任意自旋粒子)在粒子权重满足Re ∑Δᵢ > d-1条件下的情况,这是群平均对任意有限线性组合态绝对收敛的判据。同时将dS₂中的玻色子和费米子(整数和半整数自旋)粒子作为玩具模型进行分析。此外还识别了群平均在更小权重下仍能绝对收敛的特定态类别,并研究这些态的范数性质。

Result: 对于d>2维德西特空间,在粒子权重满足Re ∑Δᵢ > d-1的条件下,成功证明了所有N粒子态的群平均内积具有正定性。对于dS₂玩具模型也得到了类似结果。然而,在某些群平均仍能绝对收敛的小权重态类别中,发现了具有负群平均范数的态。这些负范数态的存在是该研究的一个重要发现。

Conclusion: 本研究为德西特空间量子引力的群平均方法提供了重要的数学基础,证明了在标准条件下群平均内积的正定性。但小权重态中负范数的出现揭示了微扰德西特量子引力理论的潜在复杂性,这可能对理论的物理解释和一致性产生重要影响,需要进一步研究这些负范数态在量子引力理论中的物理意义和理论后果。

Abstract: Perturbative quantum gravity about a global de Sitter (dS$_d$) background involves constraints that restrict the state of the linearized theory but which appear only at higher orders in perturbation theory. In particular, for familiar gravitational theories with $d>2$ these constraints require all charges defined by continuous de Sitter isometries to vanish in the linearized state. While standard Fock spaces have the property that the only normalizable state satisfying these constraints is the vacuum, Higuchi suggested that this lack of allowed states should be remedied by instead using the so-called group-averaging inner product. This inner product satisfies important uniqueness conditions, but its positivity has to date remained an open question. For $d > 2$, we establish this positivity here for all such states with $N$ particles (including particles of arbitrary spin) in standard global dS${}_d$ modes where the particle-weights $Δ_i$ satisfy the condition ${\rm Re} \, \sum_{i=1}^N Δ_i > d-1$, which is the criterion for group averaging to converge absolutely for arbitrary finite linear combinations of such states. We also treat the case of bosonic and fermionic (integer and half-integer spin) particles in dS$_2$ as a toy model for the more general case.
  However, we can identify certain classes of states for which group averaging continues to converge absolutely for smaller weights. Interestingly, in some cases these smaller-weight states turn out to have {\it negative} group-averaging norms. We thus also discuss the implications of this observation for perturbative de Sitter quantum gravity.

</details>


### [92] [Wrapping M5-branes on a non-spin manifold: Spin$^h$ structures and AdS$_7$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24084)
*Okan Günel,Cameron Gibson,C. N. Pope*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了七维最大规范超引力的AdS₂×M₅解,其中M₅为五维Wu流形SU(3)/SO(3)。尽管Wu流形不具有自旋结构或spin^c结构,该解仍具有超对称性,这是通过spin^h结构实现的。研究还将该解提升至十一维超引力,讨论了黑洞推广及其全息解释,并分析了相关几何拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有非平凡拓扑性质的超引力解是理论物理中的重要课题。Wu流形是一个特殊的五维流形,既不具有自旋结构也不具有spin^c结构,这使得在其上定义旋量变得困难。本研究旨在探索如何在这样的非常规几何背景上实现超对称超引力解,并研究其在AdS/CFT对偶及全息理论中的物理意义,同时深化对超对称性与拓扑结构之间微妙关系的理解。

Method: 研究采用了以下方法:(1)利用spin^h结构解决Wu流形上旋量定义问题,通过旋量携带SO(3)杨-米尔斯荷,使得普通旋量由于完整性产生的负号被半整数同位旋表示产生的另一负号所补偿;(2)构造七维最大规范超引力中形如AdS₂×M₅的解,其中M₅为Wu流形SU(3)/SO(3);(3)将该解提升至十一维超引力,得到AdS₂×M₉形式的解,其中M₉为Wu流形的单位切丛;(4)分析M₉的几何与拓扑性质,从十一维角度研究超对称性;(5)将七维解推广至黑洞解,并讨论其全息解释和紫外到红外流;(6)对已知的AdS₂×H³×H²解及其UV-IR流进行重新审视,给出流方程的精确解;(7)详细讨论AdS₂×S⁵解提升至十一维的情况,对比分析其内部空间M₉(S⁵的单位切丛)与Wu流形情况的异同;(8)构造Wu流形解的非紧对偶,其中M₅取为SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3)。

Result: 研究成功构造了七维最大规范超引力的超对称解AdS₂×(SU(3)/SO(3)),证明了即使在缺乏通常自旋结构的Wu流形上,通过spin^h结构仍可实现超对称性。该解可被提升至十一维超引力,得到AdS₂×M₉解,其中M₉为Wu流形的单位切丛。研究详细分析了M₉的几何拓扑性质,并从十一维视角验证了超对称性。成功将该解推广为黑洞解,并给出了全息解释及紫外-红外流的分析。对AdS₂×H³×H²解的重新研究发现其流方程存在精确解。通过对AdS₂×S⁵解提升至十一维的详细分析,揭示了其内部空间与Wu流形情况的相似性与差异。此外还构造了非紧对偶解AdS₂×(SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3))。

Conclusion: 本研究成功展示了在具有非常规拓扑结构(无自旋结构或spin^c结构)的流形上构造超对称超引力解的可行性,关键在于利用spin^h结构巧妙地通过杨-米尔斯荷补偿旋量完整性问题。Wu流形解及其十一维提升为研究具有非平凡拓扑的AdS解提供了新的重要例子,丰富了AdS/CFT对偶和全息理论的研究内容。黑洞推广及UV-IR流分析为理解这类解的物理性质和全息解释提供了深入洞察。通过与其他已知解(如AdS₂×S⁵)的对比分析,揭示了不同内部空间几何拓扑性质对超引力解结构的影响,为进一步探索超引力理论中几何与物理的关系奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct a solution of seven-dimensional maximal gauged supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_5$, where $M_5$ is the five-dimensional Wu manifold $SU(3)/SO(3)$. The solution is supersymmetric, despite the fact that the Wu manifold admits neither a spin structure nor a spin$^c$ structure. Rather, it admits a spin$^h$ structure, wherein the ill-definedness of ordinary spinors due to a minus sign in their holonomy is compensated by a further minus sign arising through the spinors carrying $SO(3)$ Yang-Mills charge, in a half-integer isospin representation of the $SU(2)$ double cover of the $SO(3)$. We show how the configuration can be lifted to a solution of $D=11$ supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_9$, where $M_9$ is the unit tangent bundle of the Wu manifold. We discuss the geometry and topology of $M_9$, and study the supersymmetry from the eleven-dimensional standpoint. We extend the seven-dimensional solution to a black hole, and discuss some aspects of its holographic interpretation and the UV to IR flow. We revisit a previously-known AdS$_2\times H^3\times H^2$ solution, and its UV to IR flow, showing that the flow equations in fact admit of an exact solution. Additionally, we give a detailed discussion of the lift of the AdS$_2\times S^5$ solution to $D=11$, exhibiting similarities and differences between the properties of its internal space $M_9$ (the unit tangent bundle of $S^5$) and the Wu manifold example of our main discussion. We also construct the non-compact dual of the Wu manifold solution, where $M_5$ is taken to be $SL(3,\mathbb R)/SO(3)$.

</details>


### [93] [Topological deformations of local gauge theories: a presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24111)
*Ivan Dneprov,Alexey Donchenko,Maxim Grigoriev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有不同规范结构和场内容但描述相同局域自由度的物理系统之间的关系,提出了"拓扑形变"的系统性构造方法,通过在预辛BV-AKSZ框架内对不同度数的对称性进行规范化,可以在不引入新局域自由度的情况下联系这些对偶系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理中存在许多用不同规范结构和场内容描述相同局域自由度的对偶系统(如四维标量场与2-形式规范场的对偶,单模引力中宇宙学常数成为整体自由度等)。这些系统的规范不等价形式解空间相差一个有限维扇区,且由于规范结构不同,它们的相互作用和对时空整体结构的敏感性可能显著不同。需要一个系统性的理论框架来理解和构造这些对偶关系。

Method: 在预辛BV-AKSZ(Batalin-Vilkovisky-Alexandrov-Kontsevich-Schwarz-Zaboronsky)方法框架内,通过"拓扑形变"系统性地构造对偶系统:对一般度数的对称性进行规范化,使系统耦合到BF型扇区而不引入新的局域自由度。该构造本质上是对底层预辛规范偏微分方程的修改。具体包括:(1)对0度对称性的规范化识别由整体对称性联系的解;(2)对1度对称性(即形变)的规范化产生新的整体自由度。

Result: 成功重现了已知的对偶关系:通过规范化0度对称性再现了标量场-2-形式对偶和主手征模型-Freedman-Townsend对偶;通过规范化1度对称性(形变)得到了单模引力中宇宙学常数作为整体自由度的情况。该方法在预辛BV-AKSZ框架内系统地建立了不同规范系统之间的拓扑形变关系。

Conclusion: 拓扑形变提供了一个统一的理论框架来理解和构造具有相同局域自由度但不同规范结构的对偶物理系统。在预辛BV-AKSZ方法中,通过对不同度数对称性的规范化,可以系统地在不引入新局域自由度的情况下联系这些系统,这种构造揭示了对偶系统之间的深层数学结构,并为理解规范理论中的整体自由度、对偶性及其与时空整体性质的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The same local degrees of freedom can be described by systems with different gauge structure and field content. Standard examples are the duality between the scalar field and the 2-form gauge field in four dimensions, and unimodular gravity, where the cosmological constant becomes a global degree of freedom. In a precise sense, the spaces of gauge-inequivalent formal solutions of such systems differ by a finite-dimensional sector. Moreover, because their gauge sectors are different, their consistent interactions and their sensitivity to the global structure of spacetime may differ substantially. We show that such systems are naturally related by what we call topological deformations: gauging a symmetry of generic degree couples the system to a BF-type sector without introducing new local degrees of freedom. The construction is carried out systematically within the presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach, where it amounts to a modification of the underlying presymplectic gauge PDE. Gauging degree-0 symmetries identifies solutions related by global symmetries, which reproduces the scalar--2-form and principal chiral model--Freedman--Townsend dualities. Gauging degree-1 symmetries, i.e. deformations, gives rise to new global degrees of freedom, such as the cosmological constant in unimodular gravity.

Abstract: We show that the dimensional reduction of four-dimensional Yang-Mills instantons naturally gives rise to nontrivial matter configurations in three dimensions exhibiting an unusual mechanism of gravitational screening. Starting from Euclidean Yang-Mills theory, we reduce (anti-)self-dual gauge configurations along a compact direction generated by a $U(1)$ isometry of the instanton background. Under this reduction, the gauge connection decomposes into a three-dimensional Yang-Mills field and an adjoint Higgs field, while the metric gives rise, as usual, to a dilaton and an Abelian gauge connection. For instantons on the Taub-NUT background, the reduced fields organize themselves into two mutually screening pairs: the backreaction of the Abelian sector cancels internally, and the same occurs independently in the non-Abelian sector. As a result, the full matter content supports a composite stealth configuration on flat three-dimensional space. By contrast, the dimensional reduction of instantons on the Eguchi-Hanson background yields a non-Abelian matter sector whose gravitational backreaction cancels, while the Abelian sector remains gravitationally active and therefore deforms the three-dimensional background geometry. These examples reveal a direct connection between four-dimensional (anti-)self-duality and three-dimensional BPS-type relations, and illustrate how instantonic sectors that are gravitationally invisible in four dimensions can descend into either fully composite stealth configurations or partially stealth matter configurations after dimensional reduction.

Abstract: We compute the one-loop correction to the non-minimal coupling of a scalar field theory to gravity, induced by a quartic self-interaction. Abstract: We derive an analytic formula for the expectation value of the current due to Schwinger pair-production in an arbitrary time-dependent, spatially inhomogeneous external electric field in 1+1 dimensions, as a perturbative expansion in the fermion mass $m$. We work to order $m^2$, finding contributions at order $m^0$, $m^2 \log m$ and $m^2$; there is no contribution at $O(m)$. The calculation is enabled by a new ``hybrid method'' using bosonization, where part of the calculation is performed on the fermion side and part is performed on the boson side. The fermion side allows us to define the vacuum using the free theory in the infinite past, while the boson side allows us to compute the current without regularization, since the current operator is linear in the boson field. In the special case of a spatially homogeneous background field, we also perform the calculation purely on the fermion side using adiabatic regularization. The detailed agreement of the results provides a highly non-trivial check of the duality.

Abstract: We observe an apparently universal behavior in large $N$ conformal field theory (CFT) in $d\geq 2$ dimensions: the single-trace, higher-spin gap obeys the parametric bound $Δ_{\rm gap} \lesssim (δc)^{1/d}$, where $δc$ is the central charge increment between successive theories. The origin of this bound is explained heuristically, using a generalized notion of branes that interpolate between conformal points in theory space. Seeking a more precise framework for this picture leads to the central concept of families of CFTs, which tacitly underlies various features and puzzles of AdS/CFT. We develop a working definition of families, with inspiration from families of $L$-functions in number theory, and formulate a family-level version of the gap bound. Interpreted holographically for families of AdS$_{d+1}$ vacua, this bound, when applicable, rules out Planckian scale separation, constrains the size and number of extra dimensions, and explains simple scaling patterns in string/M-theory. It also accounts for why all known large $N$ vector models have a bounded gap.

Abstract: The Schur sector of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory contains infinitely many protected operators, while the invariant polynomials of a single adjoint scalar are organized by finitely many Casimir degrees. I prove that the small $\mathcal N=4$ short multiplets built from these invariants strongly generate the associated chiral algebra, thereby establishing the $SU(N)$ case of the strong-generation conjecture of Beem et al. For every $N\ge2$, all field components associated with degrees $2,\ldots,N$ suffice, in every conformal weight and every degree of the standard relative quantum BRST cohomology. The proof brings together cyclic trace cohomology, quantum lifting, and an arbitrary-degree quartic--odd transport identity. Exact field relations and a finite-rank Newton obstruction exclude every additional lowest space, including non-scalar flavor representations; the required normal-ordering corrections and BRST primitives are derived explicitly. The resulting completeness theorem explains how a finite family of supersymmetric multiplets organizes an unbounded quantum spectrum. Every Schur class is a normal polynomial in their components and derivatives, even when its gauge-theory representative involves fermions and arbitrarily many derivatives. This supplies a uniform set of local inputs for meromorphic operator products, bounds protected state multiplicities before cancellations in an index, and gives an algebraic account of the finite-rank reorganization of protected trace operators.
