Method: 采用由零点长度激发的归一化核函数与原密度分布进行卷积正则化。对无界尖点情况解析求解卷积和包围质量;对截断密度构造具有切向压力的正则内部解并与Schwarzschild外部解匹配;对有限物质云进行卷积保持总质量并产生光滑物质尾。通过致密度界限论证视界规避,推导中心失效函数、建立因果测地完备性、导出跨临界致密度的统一光学延迟公式,识别超致密构型的稳定内部光环。分析无质量标量探测算符的非负性和半经典束缚态的泄漏抑制,并进行显式压力闭合比较。

Result: 成功构建了两种正则化方案:截断方案得到无视界、具有切向压力的内部解;有限云卷积方案保持质量守恒并产生光滑尾部。证明了致密度界限确保视界规避,建立了极端光学延迟下的因果测地完备性,发现超致密构型存在稳定内部光环。无质量标量探测算符非负,半经典束缚模式泄漏强烈抑制。压力闭合比较区分了几何性质的稳健性与闭合相关的延迟指数。普朗克尺度示例展示极端红移和延迟现象。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了Joshi-Malafarina-Narayan时空中心尖点的有效正则化方法,构建了物理上自洽的超致密无视界天体模型。该构造给出有效静态源描述而非微观物质理论或坍缩动力学分辨,几何性质稳健但核心区域的光子解释需要超越经典传播的物理机制。该工作为黑洞替代模型和量子引力效应在致密天体中的表现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We regularize the central density cusp of the Joshi--Malafarina--Narayan spacetime using a normalized kernel motivated by a zero-point length. For an unbounded cusp, the convolution and enclosed mass are obtained analytically; truncating this density gives a regular, horizonless tangential-pressure interior matched to Schwarzschild. Convolving the finite cloud instead preserves its total mass and yields a smooth matter tail. A compactness bound establishes horizon avoidance for this second prescription. We determine the central lapse, establish causal geodesic completeness despite extreme optical delays, derive a uniform optical-delay formula across the critical compactness, and identify the stable interior light ring of ultracompact configurations. The massless scalar probe operator is nonnegative, while semiclassical trapped modes exhibit strongly suppressed leakage. An explicit pressure-closure comparison separates robust geometric properties from closure-dependent delay exponents. Planck-scale examples illustrate extreme redshifts and delays, but their photon interpretation requires physics beyond classical propagation in the core. The construction specifies an effective static source, rather than a microscopic matter theory or a dynamical resolution of collapse.

</details>


### [131] [Group averaging and the geometry of physical quantum states: from normalizable solutions to solvable loop quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24027)
*Alejandro Corichi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在几何量子力学框架下研究了正则量子引力中群平均（group averaging）从运动学种子态到物理态的映射几何结构，区分了依赖于种子选择、测试空间或时钟选择的结构与正则结构（物理射线、物理几何和Dirac可观测量），并在可解的圈量子宇宙学模型中验证了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则量子引力中的物理态需要满足约束条件，但通常在运动学Hilbert空间中不可归一化。群平均方法可以从可归一化的运动学种子态构造物理态，但这一"种子到态"映射的几何结构尚未被系统阐明。需要在几何量子力学语言下理解这一映射，区分哪些结构是正则的（物理本质的），哪些依赖于技术选择（如种子、测试空间、时钟），从而为量子引力理论提供更清晰的数学基础。

Method: 1) 采用几何量子力学语言描述群平均的种子到态映射几何；2) 当平均是正交投影时，将可容许运动学射线构造为物理射线空间上的全纯向量丛，分析其零截面（嵌入的精确解）和纤维结构（严格大于规范轨道）；3) 对于分布型物理态，在显式Sobolev测试空间上进行群平均，构造代表元的仿射丛，其截面是物理射线空间的嵌入但不包含精确解；4) 将理论框架应用于可解的圈量子宇宙学模型，通过显式测试空间重现Klein-Gordon物理内积；5) 分析约束谱边缘效应、正则截面的模依赖性和Kähler等距性质，以及Page-Wootters态与Klein-Gordon解切片的分离。

Result: 1) 建立了群平均种子到态映射的完整几何描述：正交投影情形对应全纯向量丛结构，分布态情形对应仿射丛结构；2) 证明了可观测量下降到基空间的充要条件是约束平方的Hessian沿其流消失；3) 在圈量子宇宙学中成功重现Klein-Gordon物理内积；4) 发现由于标量场动量在约束中二次出现，约束谱在壳层附近存在边缘，导致正则截面具有模依赖性且非Kähler等距；5) 揭示了小宇宙的正则种子偏离壳层，边缘将Page-Wootters态与Klein-Gordon解切片分离，而理想时钟不显示这些效应。

Conclusion: 本文系统阐明了正则量子引力中群平均方法的几何结构，成功区分了正则结构（物理射线、物理几何和Dirac可观测量）与依赖于技术选择的结构（种子、测试空间、时钟）。在圈量子宇宙学应用中验证了理论框架的有效性，揭示了约束谱边缘效应对正则截面和物理态构造的重要影响。这一工作为量子引力理论提供了更清晰的数学基础，有助于理解量子约束系统中不同数学结构的物理意义和技术依赖性。

Abstract: In canonical quantum gravity the physical states solve the constraints but are typically not normalizable in the kinematical Hilbert space. Group averaging constructs them from normalizable kinematical seeds. We describe the geometry of this seed-to-state map in the language of geometric quantum mechanics.
  When averaging is an orthogonal projection, the admissible kinematical rays form a holomorphic vector bundle over the physical ray space. The embedded exact solutions form its zero section, and its fibers are strictly larger than gauge orbits. Observables descend to the base exactly when the Hessian of the squared constraint vanishes along their flow.
  For distributional physical states, group averaging on an explicit Sobolev test space yields an affine bundle of representatives. Its sections are embeddings of the physical ray space whose images contain no exact solutions. The test-space inner product singles out one of them, and narrowing the seed profile gives Weyl sequences of approximate solutions.
  We apply this to solvable loop quantum cosmology. Group averaging reproduces the Klein--Gordon physical inner product on an explicit test space. Because the scalar-field momentum enters the constraint quadratically, the constraint spectrum has an edge near the shell. The canonical section is then mode-dependent and not Kähler isometric, and the canonical seeds of small universes are biased off the shell. The edge also separates the Page--Wootters state from the slice of the Klein--Gordon solution. An ideal clock shows none of these effects.
  The results separate the canonical structures, namely physical rays, physical geometry and Dirac observables, from those that depend on a choice of seeds, test space or clock.

</details>


### [132] [Canonical currents without improvements: the QCD example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24040)
*Tomi Koivisto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出在广义平行相对论框架下,通过相对于独立平行标架定义正则Noether流,可以获得明确且守恒的能量-动量流和角动量流,解决了传统相对论场论中守恒流的改进项不确定性问题,并在QCD理论中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相对论场论中,能量-动量流和角动量流的定义存在改进项的不确定性,在平直时空中定义不唯一,在弯曲时空中仅沿度规对称性方向守恒。需要找到一种明确且普遍守恒的流定义方法,以解决这一理论上的根本性问题。

Method: 采用广义平行相对论(General Parallel Relativity)框架,相对于独立的平行标架定义正则Noether流。通过变分原理推导QCD理论中的规范不变张量、Belinfante-Rosenfeld关系和自旋面项。构造精确耦合解来验证在没有相应对称性时能量和转动荷的守恒性。

Result: 在新框架下,能量-动量流和角动量流的定义既明确又守恒,消除了传统理论中的不确定性。在QCD中成功导出规范不变的能量-动量张量和相关关系式。证明了精确耦合解具有守恒的能量和转动荷,即使缺乏相应的对称性。发现具有相反螺旋度的夸克虽然在度规和对称能量-动量张量下不可区分,但携带相反的转动荷。

Conclusion: 通过引入广义平行相对论框架和独立平行标架,成功解决了相对论场论中守恒流定义的不确定性问题。该方法不仅提供了明确且普遍守恒的能量-动量流和角动量流定义,还揭示了夸克自旋与转动荷之间的深层联系,为理解场论中的守恒律和对称性关系提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: In conventional formulations of relativistic field theory, energy-momentum and angular-momentum currents are defined only up to improvement terms in flat spacetime, and in curved spacetime they are conserved only along symmetries of the metric. When defined as canonical Noether currents relative to an independent parallel frame, as in General Parallel Relativity, they are both unambiguous and conserved. For QCD, the gauge-invariant tensor, the Belinfante-Rosenfeld relation and the spin surface term follow from the variational principle. An exact coupled solution is shown to have conserved energy and rotation charges without the corresponding symmetries. Quarks of opposite helicity are indistinguishable by the metric and the symmetric energy-momentum tensor, yet carry opposite rotation charges.

</details>


### [133] [Effect of induced frame dragging on energy extraction from axially symmetric magnetized Reissner-Nordström BH and observational benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24103)
*Ikhtiyor Eshtursunov,Chen Zhou,Sanjar Shaymatov,Pankaj Sheoran,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴对称磁化Reissner-Nordström黑洞(MRNBH)时空中通过磁重联过程提取能量的机制,揭示了黑洞电荷Q和磁场参数B对能量提取效率的影响,发现电磁感应的框架拖曳效应可以达到与旋转黑洞相当的能量提取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上,能量提取过程主要与旋转黑洞的几何能层相关联。本研究旨在探索在带电黑洞(MRNBH)时空中,电磁感应产生的框架拖曳效应是否能够支持类似的能量提取机制,特别是通过磁重联过程实现能量提取,并评估其效率是否可与极端Kerr黑洞相媲美。

Method: 首先分析轴对称MRNBH的度规,区分几何能层与感应能层的差异,强调感应能层是依赖于粒子性质的负能量区域而非时空度规的固有属性。然后确定磁重联能够从MRNBH提取能量的区域及其所需条件,计算加速和减速等离子体流的能量以及相应的磁重联效率。最后,基于减速等离子体在无穷远处的能量评估重联功率,并与M87*和Sgr A*的观测功率尺度进行比较。

Result: 研究发现,在所考虑的参数范围内,能量提取效率随着MRNBH电荷Q和磁场参数B的增加而提高。当磁场参数B足够大时,提取效率可达到与极端Kerr黑洞相当的水平。对于Q=M的情况,只有当|B|M≳0.2(对应M87*约10^9 G,Sgr A*约10^12 G)时才出现重联提取现象,此时提取功率P_ext~10^53-10^57 erg/s,比观测到的功率尺度高出许多数量级。

Conclusion: 电磁感应的框架拖曳效应可以支持与旋转参数相关的能量提取效率相当的机制。带电黑洞通过磁重联过程提取能量的效率与黑洞电荷和磁场强度密切相关,在强磁场条件下能达到极高的能量提取率。然而,理论预测的提取功率远超M87*和Sgr A*的观测值,这为理解实际天体物理黑洞的能量提取机制提供了重要的理论参考和约束条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we probe the effect of induced frame dragging on the energy extraction process via magnetic reconnection in axially symmetric magnetized Reissner-Nordström black hole (MRNBH) spacetime and show the influence of BH charge $Q$ and the magnetic field $B$ parameters, respectively. We start by analyzing the metric and present the distinction between the geometrical and induced ergospheres. We argue that the induced ergosphere, unlike the geometric ergosphere, is not a property of the spacetime metric but rather a particle-dependent negative energy region that changes as the charge on the particle varies. We find the region where magnetic reconnection can extract energy from the axially symmetric MRNBH and determine the conditions required for this process. We further examine the energies of the accelerated and decelerated plasma outflows and the corresponding magnetic reconnection efficiency. We show that within the parameter ranges considered, the extraction efficiency increases with the MRNBH charge $Q$ and the magnetic field parameter $B$. For sufficiently large $B$, it reaches the efficiency comparable to that of an extremal Kerr BH case. Our results therefore show that electromagnetically induced frame dragging can support energy extraction efficiencies similar to those associated with rotation parameter. To connect with observations, we evaluate the reconnection power directly from the decelerated-plasma energy at infinity and compare it with the observed power scales of M87* and Sgr~A*. For $Q=M$, reconnection extraction appears in our scan only for $|B|M\gtrsim0.2$ ($B\sim10^{9}\,\mathrm{G}$ for M87*, $\sim10^{12}\,\mathrm{G}$ for Sgr~A*), where $P_{\mathrm{ext}}\sim10^{53}$--$10^{57}\,\mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$ exceeds the observed power scales by many orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [134] [How accurate is Spectral Einstein Code (SpEC)? And what limits its accuracy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24283)
*Himanshu Chaudhary,Mark A. Scheel,Robert Owen,Saul A. Teukolsky,Michael Boyle,Lawrence E. Kidder,Harald P. Pfeiffer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用谱爱因斯坦代码(SpEC)进行双黑洞(BBH)模拟时的误差来源,特别关注耗时最长的旋近阶段,并通过优化模拟参数和分辨率降低了约束违反和引力波波形差异,同时指出浮点误差和基本变量尺度差异是高分辨率下的根本性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来引力波探测器灵敏度的提高要求更高精度的双黑洞模拟,但这类模拟计算成本极高。因此需要理解模拟的误差来源并高效利用计算资源,以满足下一代引力波观测的精度需求。

Method: 使用谱爱因斯坦代码(SpEC)对双黑洞系统进行数值模拟,重点分析旋近阶段的误差。通过针对性地调整计算域不同部分的模拟参数和分辨率,系统性地研究约束违反和引力波波形差异。在更高分辨率下,进一步分析浮点运算误差和基本变量尺度差异等根本性限制因素。

Result: 通过有针对性地改变计算域不同部分的模拟参数和分辨率,成功降低了约束违反程度和引力波波形差异。识别出在高分辨率情况下影响精度的两个根本性限制:浮点运算误差和基本变量之间的大尺度差异。这些限制的存在意味着进一步提高精度需要对代码和演化方程的表述进行实质性改进。

Conclusion: 通过优化SpEC代码的模拟参数和分辨率配置,可以有效提高双黑洞模拟在旋近阶段的精度。然而,浮点误差和变量尺度差异构成了更深层次的精度瓶颈,解决这些问题需要对SpEC代码本身和爱因斯坦方程的演化表述进行重大修改。论文中讨论的改进方法和面临的挑战对其他数值相对论代码同样具有参考价值。

Abstract: The improved sensitivity of upcoming gravitational-wave detectors will require correspondingly more accurate binary black hole (BBH) simulations. Given the high cost of such simulations, it is essential to understand their sources of error and use computational resources efficiently. We study sources of error in BBH simulations performed with the Spectral Einstein Code (SpEC) and seek to understand what limits their accuracy. In particular, we focus on the inspiral phase because it is the longest-running part of the simulation and accounts for the majority of the waveform error. We find that targeted changes to simulation parameters and resolution in different parts of the computational domain reduce constraint violations and gravitational waveform differences. We also discuss more fundamental limitations on our accuracy that become important at higher resolutions: floating-point errors and large differences in the scales of the fundamental variables. Addressing these limitations will require substantial changes to both the SpEC code and the formulation it uses to evolve Einstein's equations. Many of the improvements and changes discussed in this paper are likely to be relevant to other numerical relativity codes.

</details>


### [135] [Linearised gravitational radiation in de Sitter spacetime at all multipole orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24303)
*Harsh,Omkar Shetye,Amitabh Virmani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了德西特时空中局域物质源在任意多极阶产生的线性化引力波，通过映射到辅助平坦时空问题，利用Damour-Iyer多极展开方法获得了外部度规的显式表达式，其中德西特尾效应被完全吸收到辅助应力张量中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在德西特时空背景下研究引力波的多极展开是一个重要但复杂的问题。传统方法中，德西特时空的尾效应会导致引力波场包含源的整个历史积分，使得计算变得复杂。需要发展一种新的形式化方法，能够在任意多极阶系统地处理德西特时空中的线性化引力波问题，同时避免历史积分的复杂性。

Method: 将德西特时空Poincaré坐标系中的线性化引力波问题精确映射到调和规范下的辅助平坦时空问题。构造一个守恒的辅助应力张量来描述物理源，使得可以直接应用Damour和Iyer的多极展开方法。德西特尾效应被完全吸收进辅助应力张量中，从而辐射多极矩和正则外部度规都不再包含源过去历史的积分。通过将多极矩表示为物理应力张量的显式积分，在任意多极阶上给出外部度规的完整表达式。

Result: 成功推导出德西特时空中任意多极阶的线性化引力波外部度规，用两族辐射多极矩的显式积分形式表示。这些多极矩和正则场均不包含历史积分项，大大简化了计算。作为验证，在四极近似下恢复了已知的四极度规，并显式构造了将正则场变换到已知形式的剩余规范变换，证实了方法的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了处理德西特时空中引力波多极展开的系统方法，通过映射到辅助平坦时空问题并巧妙地将尾效应吸收到辅助应力张量中，实现了任意多极阶引力波的简洁表述。该方法不仅在理论上更加优雅（避免了历史积分），而且在实际计算中更加高效，为研究宇宙学背景下的引力波辐射提供了强有力的数学工具。

Abstract: We derive the linearised gravitational waves generated by a localised matter source in the Poincare patch of de Sitter spacetime at arbitrary multipole order. The linearised de Sitter problem is mapped exactly to an auxiliary flat-space problem in harmonic gauge, with a conserved source built from the physical stress tensor. This allows us to use the multipole expansion of Damour and Iyer verbatim, and yields the exterior metric at all orders in terms of two families of radiative multipole moments given as explicit integrals of the physical stress tensor. The de Sitter tail is absorbed entirely into the auxiliary stress tensor, so that neither the moments nor the canonical exterior metric carry an integral over the past history of the source. As a check, we recover the known quadrupolar metric: in the quadrupolar truncation it is our canonical field up to a residual gauge transformation, which we construct explicitly.

</details>


### [136] [Grey-body factors of black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24356)
*Roman Konoplya,Alexander Zhidenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是关于黑洞物理中灰体因子的综述性论文。灰体因子描述了场通过黑洞或致密天体产生的有效势垒时的频率依赖性透射特性,它将理想化的霍金辐射热谱转换为观测者测量的实际谱,并与准正则模式互补。文章系统阐述了灰体因子的理论框架、计算方法及其在不同时空背景和引力理论中的最新研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰体因子是连接黑洞理论预测与实际观测的关键物理量,但相关研究分散在不同领域。需要一个统一的理论框架来系统梳理灰体因子在黑洞物理中的作用、计算方法以及在不同时空(渐近平直、德西特、反德西特空间)和修正引力理论中的表现,同时探讨其与准正则模式、霍金辐射、吸收截面等物理过程的内在联系。

Method: 本文采用综述方法,系统整合了灰体因子研究的理论框架和计算技术。具体包括:1)建立不同渐近时空(平直、德西特、反德西特)中的散射问题形式化描述;2)阐明透射系数、吸收截面、霍金辐射和铃宕(ringdown)之间的理论联系;3)回顾各种计算方法;4)综述近期研究进展,包括灰体因子与准正则模式的对应关系、有效势垒形变下的稳定性、虫洞透射与超辐射现象、修正引力理论中的行为;5)讨论灰体因子在规范/引力对偶中的应用,将体散射概率与边界理论的关联函数和输运系数联系起来。

Result: 文章系统呈现了灰体因子研究的完整图景,包括:建立了渐近平直、德西特和反德西特时空中灰体因子的统一理论框架;阐明了灰体因子与准正则模式、霍金辐射、吸收截面等观测量之间的内在联系;总结了有效的计算方法;揭示了灰体因子在有效势垒形变下的稳定性特征;展示了其在虫洞、超辐射、修正引力理论等扩展场景中的表现;建立了通过规范/引力对偶将灰体因子与边界场论物理量(关联函数、输运系数)联系的理论桥梁。

Conclusion: 本综述为灰体因子研究提供了一个统一且全面的理论框架。灰体因子不仅是将理想黑洞辐射理论与实际观测连接的关键工具,还是探测黑洞时空结构、检验引力理论的重要实频域观测量。文章展示了灰体因子与准正则模式的深刻对应关系,揭示了其在不同时空背景和修正引力理论中的普适性和稳定性。通过规范/引力对偶,灰体因子还为理解强耦合场论的输运性质提供了几何视角。这些结果为未来黑洞物理的理论研究和引力波天文观测提供了重要的理论基础和计算工具。

Abstract: Grey-body factors describe the frequency-dependent transmission of fields through the effective potential generated by a black hole or another compact object. They convert the idealized thermal spectrum of Hawking radiation into the spectrum measured by an observer, determine absorption and scattering cross sections, and provide real-frequency observables complementary to quasinormal modes. In this review, we present a unified account of grey-body factors in black-hole physics and related systems. We formulate the scattering problem for asymptotically flat, de Sitter, and anti-de Sitter spacetimes, and discuss the connections between transmission coefficients, absorption cross sections, Hawking radiation, and ringdown. We also review methods for their computation and survey recent developments, including the correspondence between grey-body factors and quasinormal modes, their stability under deformations of the effective potential, wormhole transmission and superradiance, and their behavior in modified theories of gravity. Finally, we discuss the appearance of grey-body factors in gauge/gravity duality, where absorption probabilities in the bulk are related to correlation functions and transport coefficients in the boundary theory.

</details>


### [137] [Observational constraints on scalar-nonmetricity cosmology: the role of the connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24402)
*Ghulam Murtaza,Avik De,Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了标量-非度量引力理论中三种不对称远程连接分支的观测特性,利用超新星、重子声波振荡和宇宙微波背景数据进行约束,发现连接分支I在DES Y5+CMB+DESI DR2数据组合下相对于ΛCDM模型表现出观测优势(Δχ²=-13.99),并被Akaike信息准则所青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量-非度量引力理论是对称远程平行引力的自然标量-张量推广,其独特之处在于即使对于空间平坦的Friedmann-Robertson-Walker几何,也允许存在三种不等价的对称远程平行连接分支。这种几何自由度是否具有可观测后果是一个重要问题,因此需要对这三种连接分支进行系统的观测比较,以确定它们的物理可区分性和观测可行性。

Method: 研究采用陡峭标量场势和幂律非最小耦合的模型框架,系统地对三种连接分支进行观测约束。使用的观测数据包括:Pantheon Plus和DES Year 5 Ia型超新星数据的不同组合、DESI DR2重子声波振荡数据,以及压缩的宇宙微波背景信息。通过联合拟合这些数据集,计算各连接分支的χ²统计量和Akaike信息准则(AIC),以评估模型相对于ΛCDM宇宙学的表现。

Result: 三种连接分支都能够重现从物质主导到晚期加速的宇宙学转变,但其观测行为依赖于具体的连接选择。连接分支I对超新星数据编纂表现出最强的敏感性;连接分支II给出了非常稳定的重构宇宙学历史;连接分支III则表现出介于两者之间的行为。最显著的结果是,对于DES Y5+CMB+DESI DR2数据组合,连接分支I相对于ΛCDM模型给出Δχ²_min=-13.99和ΔAIC=-5.99,表明其拟合质量更优。

Conclusion: 研究证明了标量-非度量引力理论中的几何自由度具有可观测的后果,不同连接分支展现出可区分的观测特征。连接分支I在当前最新观测数据的联合约束下被Akaike信息准则所青睐,相对于标准ΛCDM宇宙学模型表现出统计学上的优势。这表明对称远程平行引力框架中的连接选择不仅是形式上的数学自由度,而且可能具有真实的物理意义,为理解宇宙晚期加速提供了一个值得进一步探索的理论方向。

Abstract: Scalar-nonmetricity gravity provides a natural scalar-tensor extension of symmetric teleparallel gravity, with the gravitational interaction encoded in nonmetricity. A distinctive feature is that, even for a spatially flat Friedmann-Robertson-Walker geometry, three inequivalent symmetric-teleparallel connection branches are allowed. Whether this geometrical freedom has observable consequences is therefore an important question. In this work we perform a systematic observational comparison of all three connection branches, considering a steep scalar-field potential and a power-law nonminimal coupling. We constrain the models using combinations of Pantheon Plus and DES Year 5 Type Ia supernovae, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations, and compressed cosmic microwave background information. We find that all three branches can reproduce the transition from matter domination to late-time acceleration, but their observational behavior is connection dependent. Connection I exhibits the strongest sensitivity to the supernova compilation, Connection II yields remarkably stable reconstructed histories, while Connection III shows an intermediate behavior. Most notably, for DES Y5+CMB+DESI DR2, Connection I gives $Δχ^2_{\min}=-13.99$ and $Δ{\rm AIC}=-5.99$ relative to $Λ$CDM, and is therefore favored by the Akaike information criterion.

</details>


### [138] [Static tidal Love numbers of Schwarzschild and non-Schwarzschild black holes in quadratic gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24625)
*Soham Bhattacharyya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了四维二次引力理论中黑洞的静态电四极响应，发现非Schwarzschild解具有非零负响应，并通过数值方法给出了Love数的精确值，解释了背景解的shooting方法和潮汐解的匹配技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在四维二次引力（包含$C^2$和$R^2$项的高阶修正）中，研究黑洞的潮汐Love数对理解引力理论的修正效应至关重要。虽然Schwarzschild黑洞在广义相对论中具有零Love数，但在高阶引力理论中，非Schwarzschild解的潮汐响应特性尚未明确，需要通过严格的数值和解析方法来确定这些修正理论中黑洞的电四极响应。

Method: 采用Kodama-Ishibashi规范不变变量描述扰动，通过视界正则性条件和无外加标量潮汐的约束排除标量曲率扰动后，剩余静态扇区包含三个耦合的二阶径向方程。使用shooting方法获得背景解，通过在中间半径处的匹配来选择潮汐解。定义方程中的曲率振幅并分析其耦合特性，进行耦合扫描以研究响应随参数的变化规律。

Result: 在参数$α/r_h^2=1/2$处，数值计算得到电四极潮汐Love数$λ_E/r_h^5\simeq-0.1224928$，相应的无量纲Love数$k_2^{(r_h)}\simeq-0.1837391$，外端点处$k_2^{(r_h)}$的最终变化约为$1.60\times10^{-7}$。耦合扫描显示负响应在接近Schwarzschild交点附近趋近于零。数值结果确认了非Schwarzschild黑洞解具有非零的负四极响应，这与Schwarzschild解的零Love数形成对比。

Conclusion: 四维二次引力理论中的非Schwarzschild黑洞具有非零负的静态电四极Love数，这一完整理论结果与低能微扰展开中移除曲率平方算符的做法不冲突。研究揭示了高阶引力修正对黑洞潮汐响应的显著影响，为理解修正引力理论中的黑洞性质提供了重要的数值和理论依据。耦合方程的复杂性说明简单的分离尝试会失败，需要采用完整的数值匹配方法。

Abstract: We calculate the static electric quadrupolar response of black holes in four-dimensional quadratic gravity, with action $R-αC^2+βR^2$. The Schwarzschild solution has zero massless gravitational Love number under the stated boundary conditions. A numerical non-Schwarzschild solution instead has a nonzero negative response. At $α/r_h^2=1/2$, we find $λ_E/r_h^5\simeq-0.1224928$ and $k_2^{(r_h)}\simeq-0.1837391$, with a final outer-endpoint change in $k_2^{(r_h)}$ of about $1.60\times10^{-7}$. We explain how the background is obtained by shooting and how the tidal solution is selected by matching at an intermediate radius. The perturbations are written in Kodama--Ishibashi gauge-invariant variables. After the scalar-curvature perturbation is excluded by horizon regularity and the absence of an applied scalar tide, the remaining static sector has three coupled second-order radial equations. We define the curvature amplitudes in these equations and describe why simple attempts to separate them fail. The coupling scan shows a negative response approaching zero near the Schwarzschild intersection. We also explain why this full-theory result does not conflict with the removal of curvature-squared operators in a perturbative low-energy expansion.

</details>


### [139] [Radius of the innermost stable circular orbit of black-holes with localized Astrophysical Dark Matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24673)
*Anup S. Karekar,Siba Prasad Das*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了被局域化天体物理暗物质晕包围的静态球对称黑洞的测地线结构，通过数值求解爱因斯坦场方程，发现暗物质晕会使类时最内稳定圆轨道半径（r_ISCO）相对于史瓦西黑洞向内移动，且晕核半径越大位移越明显，为通过高能天体物理观测探测黑洞毛发提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑洞研究多基于真空解（无毛黑洞），但实际天体物理环境中黑洞周围存在暗物质晕。为探究暗物质晕对黑洞时空结构的影响，特别是对粒子轨道稳定性的作用，需要建立包含局域化天体物理暗物质（ADM）晕的黑洞模型，并研究其测地线结构，以期通过高能天体物理观测手段探测黑洞的"毛发"特征。

Method: 采用核半径正则化的密度分布模型描述暗物质晕，该密度在事件视界处为零且通过指数因子保证总质量有限；使用状态方程p=wρ（硬物质情形w=1）数值求解爱因斯坦场方程；通过分析有效势及其一阶、二阶导数，计算有毛黑洞附近大质量粒子的类时最内稳定圆轨道特征半径r_ISCO；对不同晕核半径r_0参数进行数值分析。

Result: 数值结果与最近提出的理论上界r_ISCO≤6（归一化到单位质量黑洞）一致；发现ADM暗物质晕使r_ISCO相对于史瓦西黑洞（无毛情形）向内移动，且晕核半径越大位移越显著；具体数值为：当r_0=2.0时r_ISCO≈5.97，r_0=10.0时r_ISCO≈5.61，r_0=20.0时r_ISCO≈5.53。

Conclusion: 本研究证明局域化天体物理暗物质晕会显著改变黑洞周围粒子的最内稳定圆轨道位置，使其向内移动，且这种效应随暗物质晕核半径增大而增强。该ADM暗物质晕模型的分析为通过高能天体物理观测量（如X射线双星、吸积盘辐射等）探测黑洞毛发特征提供了理论基础，有助于区分真空黑洞与暗物质环境中的黑洞。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure of a static, spherically symmetric black-hole in the
  background of a localized Astrophysical Dark Matter (ADM)-halo. The halo is modeled by a
  core-radius ($r_0$), regularized density profile that vanishes at the event horizon
  and exponential factor ensures the finite total halo mass. We solved the Einstein field
  equations numerically with equation of state with $p=wρ$ for stiff matter $w=1$ scenario.
  By analyzing the effective potential and its first and second derivatives, we evaluated the
  characteristic radius of the time-like innermost stable circular orbit ($r_{\text{ISCO}}$)
  of massive particle in the vicinity of hairy black-hole. Our numerical analysis is consistent
  with the recently proposed theoretical upper bound $r_{\text{ISCO}} \le 6$ normalized to
  unit mass black-hole. We showed that the ADM-halo shifts $r_{\text{ISCO}}$ inward with respect
  to the Schwarzschild (bald) scenario and the shifts are larger for larger halo core-radius.
  In particular our numerical finding enumerate $r_{\text{ISCO}}$ are approximately
  $5.97$, $5.61$, $5.53$ for $r_0=2.0,10.0,20.0$ respectively. We believe that our analysis
  with ADM-halo model shed light on probing black-hole hair through high-energy
  astrophysical observables.

</details>


### [140] [Complexification of the Kerr-AdS black hole thermodynamic phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24694)
*Fei Guo,Zhen-Ming Xu,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个系统的复分析框架来研究高维单旋转Kerr-AdS黑洞的热力学性质，通过将黑洞视界半径解析延拓到复平面，将热力学行为映射到解析函数的零点分布，并基于Lee-Yang相变理论构造Widom线来刻画超临界交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的黑洞热力学分析方法存在局限性，需要一种新的系统性理论框架来深入理解高维旋转黑洞的相变行为，特别是临界点以下的相变判据和超临界区域的交叉行为。通过引入复分析方法和Lee-Yang相变理论，可以从拓扑学角度揭示黑洞热力学的深层结构。

Method: 采用复分析框架，将黑洞视界半径进行解析延拓到复平面；通过研究对应解析函数的零点分布来映射黑洞的热力学行为；在亚临界区域建立相变识别判据；在超临界区域应用Lee-Yang相变理论构造Widom线来表征超临界交叉行为。

Result: 在临界点以下区域，该方法成功提供了识别相变的判据，并揭示了零阶相变不会改变黑洞的底层热力学拓扑结构；在超临界区域，通过构造Widom线成功刻画了Kerr-AdS黑洞的超临界交叉行为；建立了解析函数零点分布与热力学性质之间的明确对应关系。

Conclusion: 本研究通过复分析框架和Lee-Yang理论为高维单旋转Kerr-AdS黑洞的热力学分析提供了新的视角和系统性方法，揭示了相变的拓扑本质，证明了零阶相变保持热力学拓扑结构不变，并成功刻画了超临界区域的物理行为。这一框架为黑洞热力学研究开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a systematic complex-analytical framework for investigating the thermodynamics of high-dimensional singly spinning Kerr-AdS black holes. By performing an analytical continuation of the black hole horizon radius into the complex plane, we map the thermodynamic behavior for the black hole to the zeros distribution of the corresponding analytical function. In the region below the critical point, this approach provides the criterion for identifying phase transitions and reveals that the zeroth-order phase transition does not alter the underlying thermodynamic topological structure of the black hole. In the supercritical region, based on the Lee-Yang theory of phase transition, we construct the Widom line to characterize the supercritical crossover behavior for the Kerr-AdS black holes. This study provides a new perspective on the thermodynamic analysis of black holes.

</details>


### [141] [Weak Cosmic Censorship in Einstein-Born-Infeld Black Hole: A Parameter-Space Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24731)
*M. L. Peñafiel,F. T. Falciano,J. C. Fabris*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Einstein-Born-Infeld带电黑洞的宇宙监督猜想，提出了一种新的替代方法来探索其完整参数空间，避免了传统方法中难以计算的测试粒子自能问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Einstein-Born-Infeld黑洞具有独特的物理特性：它违反Bekenstein熵上界，相对于Reissner-Nordström黑洞可能过度充电，因此成为检验宇宙监督猜想的理想对象。然而，由于电磁非线性效应，传统方法需要计算黑洞附近测试粒子的自能，这在一般情况下是不可行的，因此需要开发新的分析方法。

Method: 提出了一种替代性方法（alternative approach）来绕过电磁非线性带来的测试粒子自能计算困难，使得能够系统地探索Einstein-Born-Infeld黑洞的整个参数空间。具体技术细节在摘要中未详述。

Result: 该方法能够成功探索Einstein-Born-Infeld黑洞的完整参数空间，为检验宇宙监督猜想提供了可行的分析框架。摘要中未给出具体数值结果或结论性验证数据。

Conclusion: 本研究为Einstein-Born-Infeld黑洞的宇宙监督猜想检验提供了一种新的可行方法，克服了传统方法中由于Born-Infeld非线性电动力学导致的计算障碍，使得对这类黑洞的完整参数空间分析成为可能，推进了对黑洞物理中重要猜想的验证工作。

Abstract: Einstein-Born-Infeld is a charged black hole solution of general relativity with Born-Infeld nonlinear electrodynamics, which has interesting physical features. It violates Bekenstein's entropy upper bound, and it can overcharge with respect to the Reissner-Nordström black hole. It presents an optimum situation to test the cosmic censorship conjecture, which is another important conjecture in black hole physics. In principle, this analysis requires the calculation of the self-energy of a test particle in the vicinity of the black hole, which, in general, is not feasible because of the electromagnetic nonlinearities. In this work, we propose an alternative approach that allows us to explore the whole parameter space of the Einstein-Born-Infeld black hole and test the validity of the cosmic censorship conjecture.

</details>


### [142] [Global isometric embedding of the Kerr spacetime and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24735)
*Mikhail Markov,Anton Sheykin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了Kerr时空到12维平直时空的新等距嵌入,该嵌入渐近平坦、覆盖整个r>0区域且形式简洁,并将该方法推广到任何具有Kerr-Schild型度规分解的时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找Kerr时空的等距嵌入一直是广义相对论中的重要问题。已有的嵌入方法往往存在复杂性高、覆盖区域有限或不满足渐近平坦等问题。本文旨在构造一个形式简洁、性质良好且覆盖完整的Kerr时空等距嵌入,并探索其向更一般时空的推广可能性。

Method: 采用等距嵌入理论,将4维Kerr时空嵌入到(5+7)维即12维平直时空中。利用Kerr度规的Kerr-Schild分解特性,构造嵌入函数使得诱导度规与Kerr度规完全一致。通过系统分析Kerr-Schild型度规的共性特征,将嵌入构造方法推广到所有允许类似度规分解的时空。

Result: 成功构造了Kerr时空到12维平直时空的等距嵌入,该嵌入具有以下优良性质:(1)渐近平坦;(2)覆盖整个r>0区域;(3)嵌入形式非常简洁。证明了该方法可以推广应用于任何具有Kerr-Schild型度规分解的时空,为这类时空提供了统一的嵌入方案。

Conclusion: 本文提供了Kerr时空的一个新的、性质优良的等距嵌入,在简洁性、覆盖完整性和渐近行为方面都有显著优势。更重要的是,基于Kerr-Schild分解的嵌入构造方法具有普适性,可以系统地应用于广义相对论中的一大类重要时空,为研究弯曲时空的几何结构提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We construct a new isometric embedding of the Kerr spacetime into $(5+7)$-dimensional flat spacetime. It is asymptotically flat, covers the entire $r>0$ region and has a very simple form. We also show how this embedding can be generalized to any spacetime admitting a Kerr-Schild-like decomposition of the metric.

</details>


### [143] [A Casimir turning point as the beginning of a small flat universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24905)
*Alan Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由标准模型场的负卡西米尔能量使收缩转向的平坦三环面宇宙。在特定条件下(反周期费米子、特定的希格斯耦合和顶夸克质量约束),宇宙在约722个约化普朗克长度处发生转向。该模型将此转向点解读为宇宙起源而非反弹,并预言三环面形状会冻结为近似长方体,其边长差异可通过宇宙微波背景观测验证,且可被未来的顶夸克质量精确测量所检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学模型中宇宙起源问题仍存在疑问。本文探索一种替代方案:利用标准模型场的负卡西米尔能量使收缩中的宇宙转向,从而避免传统的奇点问题。关键动机在于:(1)检验标准模型物理在极小尺度宇宙学中的作用;(2)研究痕反常对各向异性宇宙演化的约束;(3)探讨希格斯场在极端条件下的稳定性;(4)寻找可通过粒子物理实验(如顶夸克质量测量)和宇宙学观测(微波背景)检验的宇宙起源模型。

Method: 采用理论分析和数值计算相结合的方法:(1)建立平坦三环面几何框架,引入反周期边界条件的标准模型场;(2)计算卡西米尔能量和痕反常的贡献,确定宇宙转向条件;(3)分析希格斯场的零点涨落和规范场恒定模式对希格斯零模的约束效应,计算有限体积下的临界四次耦合λ_c≈-0.012;(4)通过标准模型重整化群方程和当前实验数据(顶夸克质量、强耦合常数、希格斯曲率耦合),计算希格斯存活概率;(5)研究环面形状态的量子演化,分析形状冻结机制和存活概率;(6)评估可观测效应并给出实验检验预言。

Result: (1)宇宙转向发生在边长约722个约化普朗克长度处;(2)零点涨落将希格斯四次耦合的稳定性阈值推至λ_c≈-0.012,低于标准模型值-0.0098;(3)希格斯存活概率超过1/2要求顶夸克极点质量低于172.96 GeV(MS质量<163.16 GeV,α_s(M_Z)=0.1180),当前数据下存活概率约0.7(需希格斯曲率耦合ξ_H≳0.08);(4)转向点只能容纳约5个最低模式的量子,辐射能量约束排除了时间对称的反弹图像;(5)形状态只能在一个时刻为实数,自然态在转向点为实并冻结成近似长方体,边长典型差异16-18%;(6)90%形状存活概率要求实数时刻在转向点0.35哈勃时间内;(7)最短周期需在0.985-1.04倍末次散射直径间才能在微波背景中留下印记;(8)若顶夸克极点质量超过173.7 GeV,该起源模型将被排除(贝叶斯因子≲0.03)。

Conclusion: 标准模型的负卡西米尔能量原则上可以使极小尺度的收缩宇宙转向,但这一机制面临严格约束。该模型更适合解读为宇宙起源而非反弹,因为能量密度限制排除了时间对称的热辐射前史。形状冻结机制预言宇宙具有各向异性的三环面拓扑结构,这一化石特征可能通过宇宙微波背景观测到。该模型具有高度可检验性:未来的FCC-ee实验若测得顶夸克质量高于173.7 GeV即可排除该起源机制。这展示了粒子物理与宇宙学深度交织的一个具体范例,将极早期宇宙的量子引力效应与当前可测量的标准模型参数紧密联系起来。

Abstract: We study a flat three-torus universe whose contraction is turned around by the negative Casimir energy of Standard Model fields. The trace anomaly forbids conformal matter alone to turn an anisotropic universe around, so matter with $w<1/3$, most cheaply a positive potential, must supply the negative trace; with antiperiodic fermions the Standard Model supplies the negative energy, and the universe turns at an edge of 722 reduced Planck lengths. There the Higgs quartic coupling is negative, but zero-point fluctuations of the constant gauge modes confine the Higgs zero mode down to a finite-volume threshold $λ_c\approx-0.012$, below the Standard Model value $-0.0098$. The Higgs survives with probability above $1/2$ for a top-quark pole mass below $172.96$ GeV ($\bar m_t<163.16$ GeV; $α_s(M_Z)=0.1180$), and with probability $\approx0.7$ for current data, given a curvature coupling $ξ_H\gtrsim0.08$ at the scale $\bar M_{\mathrm{P}}/722$. The turning point can hold only about five quanta of the lowest modes, whereas a reheating coupling in a time-symmetric past would produce radiation exceeding the Casimir energy by at least $10^{94}$, so we read the turn as a beginning rather than a bounce. Negative Casimir energy cannot hold the shape of the torus: the shape state can be real at most once, and for a real Gaussian state survival of the shape on both branches with $90\%$ probability requires that moment to lie within $0.35$ Hubble times of the turn. The natural state, real at the turn, freezes the torus into a nearly rectangular box whose sides typically differ by $16$-$18\%$. The microwave background can read this fossil only if the shortest cycle lies between $0.985$ and about $1.04$ last-scattering diameters. A top-quark pole mass above $173.7$ GeV, within reach of FCC-ee, would exclude this beginning (Bayes factor $\lesssim0.03$).

</details>


### [144] [Scalar fields in the background of a linear Vaidya spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24909)
*Arielle Schutz,Ratindranath Akhoury*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在半经典准静态近似下研究了由线性衰减质量的Vaidya度规描述的动态黑洞视界附近无质量标量场的纠缠性质,发现蒸发导致的共形因子会在真空态中引入相位变化,并通过CHSH不等式量化了纠缠变化,还分析了引力坍缩形成的Vaidya黑洞的近视界结构偏离永恒黑洞的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永恒黑洞的纠缠性质已被广泛研究,但真实黑洞会经历蒸发和质量变化。需要理解动态黑洞(特别是质量衰减的Vaidya黑洞)的视界附近量子场的纠缠性质如何偏离静态情形,以及蒸发过程对真空态纠缠结构的具体影响,这对理解黑洞信息悖论和霍金辐射的量子特性至关重要。

Method: 采用半经典准静态近似方法,在近视界极限下利用共形等价性和模理论分析真空纠缠;通过研究共形因子对Bogoliubov变换的修正,揭示蒸发引入的相位变化;使用CHSH不等式作为量化工具测量纠缠变化;针对引力坍缩形成的线性Vaidya黑洞,改编Wald对Schwarzschild黑洞的分析方法,研究近视界结构和态的修正。

Result: 对于质量线性衰减的Vaidya黑洞,共形因子修正了Bogoliubov变换,在类热场双态结构的全局真空中引入了相位,该相位变化源于视界的绝热漂移;CHSH关联量相比静态黑洞减小了一个依赖于蒸发率的因子;对于引力坍缩形成的线性Vaidya黑洞,近视界结构和态不趋近于永恒黑洞,热因子被包含质量变化的修正因子改变,全局纠缠真空态仍具有修正的热场双态结构。

Conclusion: 动态黑洞的蒸发过程通过共形因子对视界附近量子场的纠缠性质产生可量化的影响:在近视界极限下,蒸发导致真空态获得依赖于蒸发率的相位修正,使CHSH关联减弱;引力坍缩形成的蒸发黑洞的近视界结构显著偏离永恒黑洞模型,但仍保持修正的热场双态纠缠结构。这些结果深化了对动态黑洞量子纠缠特性的理解,为研究黑洞蒸发的量子信息效应提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study, in a semiclassical, quasi-stationary approximation, the entanglement properties of massless scalar fields near the event horizon of a dynamical black hole modeled by an ingoing Vaidya metric with a linearly decaying mass. In the near-horizon limit, the geometry becomes conformally equivalent to an eternal black hole, allowing the use of modular theory to analyze vacuum entanglement. We first study the modifications to the entanglement across the bifurcation surface of such a black hole from that of an eternal one. The conformal factor accounting for the evaporation modifies the Bogoliubov transformation, inducing a phase in the thermofield-double-like structure of the global vacuum. This phase change of the vacuum state is a consequence of the adiabatic shift of the horizon. A quantitative measure of the entanglement change due to the phase is obtained through the Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt (CHSH) inequality. We compare the CHSH inequalities in the vacuum states for the static and the linear Vaidya black holes and find that the CHSH correlation quantity for the latter is decreased by a factor depending on the evaporation rate.
  Next, we discuss the changes that must be considered for the linear Vaidya black hole formed from gravitational collapse. For this purpose we adapt an analysis by Wald for the case of the Schwarzschild black hole. We find, as expected, the near-horizon structure and state do not approach that of the eternal black hole. Rather, the thermal factor is modified by a factor incorporating the change in the black hole mass due to evaporation. The entangled global vacuum state again has a thermofield double structure with the modification mentioned above.

</details>


### [145] [Bayesian Inference of Primary Hair from Black Hole Shadows in Generalized Proca Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24996)
*Jiao-Zhen She,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Proca理论中具有旋转矢量毛发的黑洞阴影及其观测约束。通过数值射线追踪构建阴影图像,并利用事件视界望远镜对M87*和Sgr A*的观测数据,使用贝叶斯推断对矢量毛发、自旋和观测倾角进行联合约束,将初级矢量毛发的光学特征与视界尺度约束定量联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Proca理论中的旋转黑洞解具有初级矢量毛发,这种毛发对黑洞阴影和光学观测特征的影响尚未被系统研究。事件视界望远镜对M87*和Sgr A*的观测提供了前所未有的视界尺度数据,为检验超越Kerr度规的替代引力理论提供了机会,因此有必要将矢量毛发的理论预言与实际观测进行定量比较。

Method: 首先在广义Proca理论框架下选择特定耦合参数切片,得到一个单参数的Kerr度规形变,该形变依赖于自旋和纬度且非圆对称。通过确定允许正则外视界存在的参数域,采用数值射线追踪技术构建黑洞阴影和引力透镜图像。利用事件视界望远镜对M87*和Sgr A*的观测数据(阴影的面积等效半径和轴比),通过贝叶斯推断方法对矢量毛发参数、黑洞自旋和观测倾角进行联合约束分析。

Result: 射线追踪图像显示黑洞阴影出现适度增大,周围的引力透镜图案也发生变化。对M87*的贝叶斯分析表明其后验分布倾向于相对较低的自旋值,并呈现出明显的自旋-矢量毛发反相关性。对Sgr A*的分析给出了自旋和矢量毛发的上限约束,但其观测倾角基本上未受约束。在非旋转极限下形变消失,但矢量场本身仍保持非平凡结构。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将广义Proca理论中初级矢量毛发的光学特征与视界尺度的定量观测约束联系起来。事件视界望远镜的观测数据能够对矢量毛发参数提供有效约束,M87*和Sgr A*表现出不同的约束特性,其中M87*显示出自旋与矢量毛发之间的反相关关系。这些结果为利用黑洞阴影观测检验修正引力理论和探测黑洞毛发提供了重要的方法学基础和实证支持。

Abstract: We investigate black hole shadows and observational constraints on primary vector hair in an exact rotating solution of generalized Proca theory. Selecting a coupling slice yields a simple, one-parameter Kerr deformation that is noncircular and depends on spin and latitude. The deformation vanishes in the nonrotating limit, while the vector field remains nontrivial. We determine the domain admitting a regular outer horizon and construct shadow and lensing images by numerical ray tracing. Ray-traced images show a modest enlargement of the shadow and changes in the surrounding lensing pattern. Bayesian inference using the shadow area-equivalent radius and axis ratio yields joint constraints on hair, spin, and viewing inclination from Event Horizon Telescope observations of M87* and Sgr A*. The M87* posterior favors relatively low spin and exhibits a pronounced spin--hair anticorrelation, while Sgr A* yields upper limits on spin and hair with its inclination remaining essentially unconstrained. These results connect the optical signatures of primary vector hair to quantitative horizon-scale constraints.

</details>


### [146] [Critical limits of accelerating Kerr--NUT spacetimes and the non-expanding Plebański--Demiański family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25031)
*Kenneth Hong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有有限非零NUT参数的真空加速Kerr-NUT时空的退化视界极限，发现在三次临界条件下，近视界极限与旋转宇宙局部同胚，并通过显式坐标变换将极端、Nariai和三次近视界度规统一到Plebański-Demiański度规族的不同曲率扇区中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索具有NUT参数的加速Kerr时空在退化视界极限下的几何结构和性质，特别是在三次临界条件($a^2=m^2+\ell^2$且$αm=1$)下的特殊行为。这类极限研究有助于理解黑洞时空的临界现象和近视界几何的普适性质，以及不同极端时空之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用射影坐标变换方法研究临界度规之间的局部同胚关系；通过构造显式四维坐标变换，将普通极端、Nariai和三次近视界度规映射到Plebański-Demiański度规族的负曲率、正曲率和零曲率纵向扇区；引入三次适配的近临界标度分析，研究接近视界之间固定一阶分离对主导度规的影响；针对Nariai对应中的例外端点构造单独的Killing坐标卡。

Result: 在三次临界条件下，三次视界具有一个有限轴和一个非紧致角向端；其近视界极限与旋转宇宙(swirling universe)局部同胚；通过射影坐标变换，完整的临界母度规通过局部同胚相关联；成功建立了三种近视界度规(普通极端、Nariai、三次)与Plebański-Demiański非膨胀度规族不同曲率扇区的明确对应关系；在三次适配标度下，接近视界间的固定一阶分离不影响主导度规项。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了加速Kerr-NUT时空在三次临界条件下的丰富几何结构，建立了不同极端近视界几何与Plebański-Demiański度规族的系统联系，证明了三次近视界极限与旋转宇宙的局部同胚性。然而，临界母度规的全局Killing辨识和正则完备化问题仍未解决，需要进一步研究。这些结果为理解黑洞时空的临界行为和极端几何的分类提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study degenerate-horizon limits of vacuum accelerating Kerr--NUT spacetimes with finite, nonzero NUT parameter. At cubic criticality, $a^2=m^2+\ell^2$ and $αm=1$, a projective coordinate transformation relates the complete critical parent metrics by a local homothety. The cubic horizon has one finite axis and a noncompact angular end, and its near-horizon limit is locally homothetic to the swirling universe. Explicit four-dimensional coordinate transformations place the ordinary extremal, Nariai and cubic near-horizon metrics in the negative-, positive- and zero-curvature longitudinal sectors of the non-expanding Plebański--Demiański family. The Nariai correspondence includes an exceptional endpoint that requires a separate Killing chart. Under a cubic-adapted near-critical scaling, fixed first-order separations between the approaching horizons do not affect the leading metric. The global Killing identifications and regular completion of the critical parent remain open.

</details>


### [147] [Gravitational lensing effects on neutrino oscillations around a Schwarzschild black hole immersed in perfect fluid dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25053)
*Sojida Mannobova,Farruh Atamurotov,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Phongpichit Channuie,Chengxun Yuan,Chen Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被完美流体暗物质包围的静态球对称黑洞在弱引力透镜regime下的中微子味振荡，推导了协变量子力学框架下的振荡相位，发现非径向传播轨迹受暗物质参数λ调制，不同味道转换通道对暗物质贡献的敏感度不同，且路径干涉项保留了中微子绝对质量标度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中微子振荡研究较少考虑引力透镜效应与暗物质环境的耦合影响。完美流体暗物质(PFDM)模型为黑洞周围暗物质分布提供了新的理论框架，需要探究这种暗物质环境如何通过引力透镜效应改变中微子味振荡特性，特别是相干透镜路径的干涉行为，以及能否通过振荡观测获取暗物质参数和中微子绝对质量信息。

Method: 采用协变量子力学框架处理超相对论中微子在PFDM包围的静态球对称黑洞时空中的传播。针对径向和非径向传播轨迹分别推导振荡相位表达式。构建两条相干透镜路径的味道转换概率模型，分析两味和三味振荡情形下的干涉图样。系统研究PFDM参数λ对振荡极值位置、振幅、对比度的影响，考察不同中微子质量标度、透镜质量、味道通道和质量序的依赖关系。

Result: 推导结果显示径向传播的主导相位与度规函数无关,而非径向轨迹获得依赖于碰撞参数和PFDM参数λ的修正项。改变λ会移动振荡极值并改变振幅和对比度,这种效应对中微子质量标度、透镜质量、味道通道和质量序呈现非单调依赖。在所考虑的参数配置下,Pμτ通道对PFDM贡献表现出特别强的敏感性。路径干涉项保留了中微子绝对质量标度的信息,为质量测量提供了新途径。

Conclusion: 本研究表征了完美流体暗物质对引力透镜中微子振荡的修正效应,建立了暗物质参数、透镜几何与振荡观测量之间的定量联系。结果表明特定味道转换通道(如Pμτ)对暗物质环境高度敏感,且干涉图样携带中微子绝对质量信息。该工作为后续纳入退相干、平均效应、探测器分辨率以及透镜轨迹和放大率修正等更现实因素的研究奠定了理论基础,为利用天体物理中微子探测暗物质性质和测定中微子质量参数提供了新的可能途径。

Abstract: We investigate neutrino flavor oscillations in the weak gravitational-lensing regime of a static, spherically symmetric black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM). Within a covariant quantum-mechanical framework, we derive the oscillation phases for radial and non-radial propagation of ultrarelativistic neutrinos. While the leading radial phase is independent of the metric function, non-radial trajectories acquire impact-parameter-dependent corrections governed by the PFDM parameter $λ$. We construct the flavor-transition probability for two coherent lensed paths and analyze the resulting interference in two and three flavor scenarios. Varying $λ$ shifts the oscillation extrema and modifies their amplitudes and contrast, with a non-monotonic dependence on the neutrino mass scale, lens mass, flavor channel, and mass ordering. The $P_{μτ}$ channel shows particularly strong sensitivity to the PFDM contribution for the considered configurations. Moreover, the path-interference terms retain information about the absolute neutrino mass scale. These results characterize PFDM-induced modifications of lensed neutrino oscillations and provide a basis for more realistic treatments including decoherence, averaging, detector resolution, and corrections to lens trajectories and magnifications.

</details>


### [148] [Destinies of Plasmoids Formed from the Magnetic Reconnection Occurring within the Ergo Region of a Rotating Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25097)
*Ho-Yun YuChih,Ye Shen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通过磁重联从旋转黑洞提取能量的Comisso-Asenjo过程，重点分析了携带提取能量的等离子体团p_+的逃逸条件。研究发现黑洞自旋存在下限a=0.8005，低于此值能量提取无法成功，这意味着磁重联导致的黑洞自旋下降会在自旋降至约0.8005时终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Comisso-Asenjo过程理论上提供了从旋转黑洞提取能量的天体物理途径，但以往研究忽略了一个关键问题：携带提取能量的等离子体团p_+能否成功逃逸到无穷远处。成功的能量提取需要同时满足两个条件：一是通过磁重联产生负无穷远能量密度的p_-，二是相关的p_+必须能逃逸。因此需要系统考察p_+的逃逸条件，建立完整的能量提取判据。

Method: 采用测地线方法系统研究等离子体团p_+在喷射后的运动轨迹和最终归宿，分析其能否逃逸到无穷远。重新定义覆盖因子（covering factor），仅统计能够成功逃逸的p_+，该参数反映了吸积系统中成功能量提取的概率。通过分析修正后的覆盖因子随黑洞自旋和等离子体磁化强度的变化规律，确定能量提取的完整条件。

Result: 通过分析修正后的覆盖因子，确定了黑洞自旋的下限为a=0.8005。当黑洞自旋低于此临界值时，无论体等离子体的磁化强度有多高，通过磁重联进行能量提取都无法成功。这一结果表明，连续磁重联导致的黑洞自旋下降过程会在自旋降至约0.8005时自然终止。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了通过磁重联从旋转黑洞提取能量的完整物理图景，首次系统考虑了携能等离子体团的逃逸条件。发现的黑洞自旋下限a=0.8005是一个基本约束，揭示了Comisso-Asenjo过程存在内禀的自旋阈值限制。这一发现对理解黑洞自旋演化、吸积流中的能量提取机制以及旋转黑洞的长期演化具有重要意义，为观测限制黑洞自旋和吸积系统物理性质提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: It is widely accepted that magnetic reconnections occur successively in black hole accretion flows, especially on the equatorial plane, owing to the formation of the equatorial current sheet. When magnetic reconnection takes place within the ergosphere of a rotating black hole, one of the ejected plasmoids (denoted as $p_-$) may acquire negative energy-at-infinity density, while the companion plasmoid (denoted as $p_+$) correspondingly carries the energy extracted from the black hole. This mechanism, known as the Comisso-Asenjo process, provides a viable astrophysical pathway for extracting energy from a rotating black hole. Generally speaking, two conditions have to be satisfied for successful energy extraction. First, $p_-$ is generated via magnetic reconnection with negative energy-at-infinity density. Second, the associated $p_+$, which carries the extracted energy, must escape to infinity. Though a number of studies have promoted the theory of the Comisso-Asenjo process, the escape of $p_+$ lacks consideration. In this work, we adopt geodesic approach to systematically examine the destinies of $p_+$ after ejection, thereby establishing the complete conditions for energy extraction from a rotating black hole via magnetic reconnection. We redefine the covering factor, for which only escaping $p_+$ are counted. This quantity reflects how probable the successful energy extraction occurs in an accretion system. Through analysis of this revised covering factor, we identify a lower bound on the black hole spin, $a=0.8005$, below which energy extraction via magnetic reconnection can never succeed, no matter how high the magnetization of the bulk plasma is. This limit suggests that the spin-down of a black hole caused by successive magnetic reconnections would terminate once the spin decreases to approximately 0.8005.

</details>


### [149] [A new regime of the C-metric: Super accelerating black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25110)
*Francisco Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文定义并研究了带电C-度规在大加速度下的超加速regime,发现极坐标需限制在更小的区间内,导致新的对称轴和锥形奇点结构变化,并在移除南极锥形缺陷后得到简洁的Komar质量表达式M_K=1/(4A),同时探索了近极端极限下的新几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电C-度规在大加速度极限下展现出特殊的时空结构,传统的分析框架不足以描述这一regime。需要系统性地定义和研究超加速regime,理解其独特的几何性质、锥形奇点结构变化以及弦/支撑解释的改变,这对于完善加速黑洞时空的理论理解具有重要意义。

Method: 通过数学分析方法研究大加速度下的带电C-度规:首先,将极坐标限制在(0,π)内的更小区间,识别出新的对称轴边界;其次,分析由此产生的锥形奇点结构变化及其对弦/支撑物理图像的影响;然后,利用Komar质量公式,在移除南极锥形缺陷的条件下推导简化的质量表达式;最后,系统探索该regime下的近极端极限情况,识别新的极限几何结构。

Result: 在超加速regime中,极坐标的限制导致了新对称轴的形成,改变了时空的锥形奇点结构和相应的弦/支撑解释。当南极的锥形缺陷被移除时,Komar质量可以简化为M_K=1/(4A)的简洁形式(需考虑时间平移Killing矢量的归一化)。在近极端极限下,识别出了多个新的极限几何结构,这些几何结构展现出与常规regime不同的性质。

Conclusion: 本研究成功定义并系统分析了带电C-度规的超加速regime,揭示了大加速度下时空结构的独特性质。极坐标限制导致的新对称轴和改变的锥形奇点结构为理解加速黑洞提供了新视角,简化的Komar质量公式M_K=1/(4A)为该regime提供了清晰的质量表征。近极端极限下新几何结构的发现进一步丰富了加速时空的几何图景,为后续研究加速黑洞的热力学性质和量子性质奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We define and investigate the super accelerating regime of the charged C-metric, which arises at large acceleration. In this regime, the polar coordinate must be restricted to a smaller interval within $(0,π)$, and the resulting boundary defines a new axis of symmetry. This changes the conical singularity structure of the spacetime and, consequently, its string/strut interpretation. We further show that, when the conical defect at the south pole is removed, the Komar mass takes the simple form $M_K=1/(4A)$, up to the normalization of the time translation Killing vector. Finally, we explore the near extremal limits of this regime, identifying new limiting geometries.

</details>


### [150] [Optical constraints on rotating logarithmic- and exponential-entropy wormholes from strong-field lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25132)
*A. Errehymy G. Yergaliyeva,S. K. Maurya,B. Turimov,I. Ibragimov,J. Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区分无视界致密天体(可穿越虫洞)与黑洞的多参数指纹方法,通过结合阴影直径、旋转不对称性、透镜系数、李雅普诺夫指数和轨道频率等5个观测量,成功打破了仅依靠阴影直径无法区分不同几何结构的简并性,实现了信噪比约3.8-3.9的判别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来,仅凭阴影直径无法区分无视界致密天体与黑洞,因为阴影直径在多种几何结构中存在简并性。现有观测手段难以仅通过单一观测量判别可穿越虫洞等替代模型与黑洞的差异,需要建立更精确的多参数判别方法来解决这一基本天体物理问题。

Method: 1) 使用新的失效函数(lapse function)插值指数型和有理型红移轮廓,精确推导慢旋转可穿越虫洞的光学表观特征;2) 从赤道零测地线出发,以闭合形式获得光子球条件、强偏转透镜系数和李雅普诺夫指数;3) 构建依赖对数和指数熵参数的两族虫洞解;4) 建立5分量指纹F=(A_rot, ā, b̄, λ_Lya, Ω_ph),包含旋转不对称性、两个透镜系数、李雅普诺夫指数和轨道频率;5) 将量子修正时空与Kerr黑洞及四种正则黑洞进行系统比较。

Result: 1) 阴影直径可调谐至Kerr黑洞的5%-11%范围内(|R_sh^WH/R_sh^Kerr - 1| ≲ 0.11),单独使用阴影无法区分模型;2) 5分量指纹的每个分量相对Kerr的分数偏差|Δ_i| ≳ 0.05,其中第二透镜系数和李雅普诺夫指数偏离最大;3) 组合判别信号对Kerr黑洞达到约3.8-3.9的信噪比,对Kazakov-Solodukhin基准达到约3.7的信噪比;4) 在10%测量精度水平下,多参数方法足以打破阴影直径简并性。

Conclusion: 单一的阴影直径观测无法有效区分可穿越虫洞与黑洞,但通过结合旋转不对称性、强偏转透镜系数、李雅普诺夫指数和轨道频率构成的5参数指纹,可以在视界尺度上实现对无视界致密天体的有效判别。该方法在当前观测精度下(约10%)能够提供足够的判别能力,为未来高精度天体物理观测区分黑洞替代模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The longstanding issue of whether horizonless compact objects can mimic black holes at horizon scales is addressed, a problem that persists because the shadow diameter alone is degenerate across a wide class of geometries. For slowly rotating traversable wormholes, we precisely formulate the optical appearance as a set of observables derived from equatorial null geodesics, yielding necessary and sufficient conditions for a multi-parameter fingerprint. Using a new lapse function that interpolates between exponential and rational redshift profiles, we obtain the photon-sphere condition, the strong-deflection lensing coefficients, and the Lyapunov exponent in closed form. The equations yield two families of wormhole solutions dependent on logarithmic and exponential entropy parameters. We compare the resulting quantum-modified spacetimes with Kerr and four regular black holes. The shadow diameter is shown to be tunable to within $5\%$ to $11\%$ of Kerr for all models considered, so that $|R_{\rm sh}^{\rm WH}/R_{\rm sh}^{\rm Kerr}-1| \lesssim 0.11$ and the shadow alone cannot separate the models. The degeneracy is lifted when the rotational asymmetry, lensing coefficients, Lyapunov exponent, and orbital frequency are combined: for the $5$-component fingerprint $\mathbf{F}=(\Arot,\abar,\bbar, \Lya,\Oph)$, the fractional deviation $Δ_i=(F_i^{\rm WH}-F_i^{\rm Kerr})/ F_i^{\rm Kerr}$ satisfies $|Δ_i|\gtrsim 0.05$ for every component, with the largest departures in the second lensing coefficient and the Lyapunov exponent. The combined discrimination signal reaches a signal-to-noise ratio of about $3.8$ to $3.9$ against Kerr and about $3.7$ against the Kazakov--Solodukhin benchmark, which is sufficient to break the shadow-diameter degeneracy at the $10\%$ measurement level.

</details>


### [151] [Photon production at Kepler escape threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25160)
*Michael R. R. Good*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对牛顿开普勒抛物线轨道进行全相对论性辐射处理,获得了总光子数的精确闭式解,证明该计数有限、尺度无关且在最近距离处的亚光速下解析,并推导出角分辨光子谱、总辐射能量和动量的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轨道辐射问题中,双曲线轨道的光子计数存在红外发散问题,而抛物线轨道作为椭圆与双曲线的临界情况,其辐射特性尚未得到精确的解析解。研究抛物线轨道的全相对论辐射处理,可以揭示轨道类型转变时辐射行为的变化规律,特别是有限性与发散性之间的过渡机制。

Method: 采用全相对论框架处理牛顿开普勒抛物线轨道的电磁辐射问题,通过解析方法推导总光子数、角分辨光子谱以及总辐射能量和动量的精确闭式表达式。分析了不同参数区域(如大快度极限、慢速运动极限)下的渐近行为,并与相邻椭圆和双曲线轨道的辐射特性进行比较分析。

Result: 获得了抛物线轨道总光子数的精确闭式解,该计数有限、尺度无关,且在最近距离处的亚光速下具有解析性。在大快度极限η下,光子数N按线性规律增长(N~(7α/√6)η),而能量E按指数增长(E∝e^(3η)),表明辐射主要表现为硬化。在慢速运动、固定耦合强度和最近距离条件下,相邻椭圆轨道每轨道周期辐射更少光子但释放更多能量和角动量。双曲线轨道在任意接近阈值时光子计数仍存在红外发散,而恰好在抛物线阈值处计数变为有限。

Conclusion: 本研究首次给出了牛顿开普勒抛物线轨道在全相对论处理下总光子数的精确闭式解,证明了抛物线阈值处光子计数的有限性,这与双曲线轨道的红外发散形成鲜明对比。研究揭示了辐射硬化的快度依赖性,以及不同轨道类型(椭圆、抛物线、双曲线)在辐射特性上的系统性差异,为理解经典带电粒子轨道辐射的临界行为提供了精确的理论基础。

Abstract: We obtain an exact closed-form total photon count for a prescribed Newtonian Kepler parabola, with a fully relativistic treatment of its radiation. We also obtain closed expressions for the angularly resolved photon spectrum and elementary expressions for the total radiated energy and momenta. The count is finite, scale-independent, and analytic in subluminal speed at closest approach. At large maximum rapidity $η$, $N\sim(7α/\sqrt6)η$, while $E\propto e^{3η}$ at fixed passage timescale: the radiation chiefly hardens. For slow motion at fixed coupling and closest distance, a nearby ellipse emits fewer photons but more energy and angular momentum per orbit. The hyperbolic photon count remains IR divergent arbitrarily close to threshold, while the count at the exact parabolic threshold is finite.

</details>


### [152] [Moving Beyond Quasinormal Modes and Reinterpreting Direct Waves in Binary Black Hole Merger-Ringdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25176)
*Richard Dyer,Anuj Kankani,Gareth Arturo Marks,Sean T. McWilliams,Christopher J. Moore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过三个独立证据链反驳了黑洞并合环降信号中存在源自视界附近的"直接波"的观点,证明早期环降信号的性质与光环而非视界相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对基于黑洞微扰理论和有效单体图像提出的双黑洞并合环降中存在可测量"直接波"、且该信号可能源自光环内部甚至接近视界区域的观点,本文旨在通过独立证据检验这些声称的合理性,明确环降信号早期成分的物理起源。

Method: 采用三个独立的证据链进行分析论证:结合黑洞微扰理论计算、准正则模式分析以及对环降信号早期时间部分的特性研究,系统考察所谓"直接波"的定义、来源及其与黑洞特征结构(光环与视界)的关联性。

Result: 确认环降信号中确实存在无法直接用准正则模式建模的早期时间成分,但该成分不符合先前定义的"直接波"特征;该早期成分的物理性质与残余黑洞的光环(light ring)相关联,而非与事件视界(event horizon)相关。

Conclusion: 通过三条独立证据链否定了环降信号中存在源自视界附近直接波的观点。虽然早期环降信号无法完全用准正则模式描述,但其物理起源应归因于光环结构而非黑洞视界,澄清了双黑洞并合环降阶段信号成分的物理本质及其空间起源。

Abstract: Based on black-hole perturbation-theory calculations and an effective-one-body picture of binary black hole mergers, it has been suggested that a direct wave may be present and measurable in the ringdown. Furthermore, it has been suggested that this signal might originate from substantially inside the light ring (or photon sphere) of the remnant black hole, perhaps even from close to the event horizon. In this paper, we present three independent strands of evidence against such claims. In the process, we show that there is indeed an early-time part of the ringdown signal that cannot be directly modeled by quasinormal modes. However, there is no evidence that this is a direct wave as it has previously been defined, and we show that the properties of this component are related to the remnant's light ring rather than its horizon.

</details>


### [153] [Probing parity-breaking black-hole geometry with extreme-mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25291)
*Zhen-Bo Wu,Qing Ou,Peng-Cheng Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进(EMRIs)系统中,宇称破缺的共形Kerr黑洞周围赤道反射对称性破缺所产生的引力波特征。通过二阶微扰分析和绝热近似,发现快速旋转黑洞会增强相位效应,在ε~10^-3时波形失配达到可探测阈值,为检验黑洞时空的反射对称性提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论预言的Kerr黑洞具有赤道反射对称性,但某些修正引力理论(如宇称破缺理论)会破坏这一对称性。极端质量比旋进系统是未来空间引力波探测器(如LISA)的重要目标源,为检验黑洞时空几何和基本对称性提供了独特机会。本研究旨在量化赤道反射对称性破缺在引力波信号中的可观测效应,特别是通过累积相位偏差来约束宇称破缺参数ε,从而检验广义相对论的有效性。

Method: 采用微扰展开方法,将宇称破缺共形Kerr黑洞的动力学参数(轨道频率、能量、极向位移等)展开到无量纲形变参数ε的二阶。利用质量四极矩近似构建绝热旋进轨道和引力波波形。分析了赤道反射对称性如何将ε映射到-ε,使得极向位移对ε为奇函数,而轨道频率和能量为偶函数。计算了一阶谐波的直接贡献和二阶动力学修正的累积效应。通过噪声加权波形失配度量和单参数Fisher分析评估对ε的探测灵敏度,研究了黑洞自旋参数a/M和观测倾角ι的影响。采用典型EMRI配置(10太阳质量小天体旋进入10^6太阳质量黑洞,观测时长一年)进行数值模拟。

Result: 研究发现:1)一阶谐波对所研究配置的直接贡献较小;2)二阶动力学修正在多个轨道周期累积后产生显著的相位偏差(dephasing);3)快速旋转黑洞(高自旋a/M)显著增强相位效应和波形失配;4)对于a/M=0.9、观测倾角ι=2π/3的一年观测,当ε≈10^-3时固定参数波形失配达到比较基准值0.01(未对时间、相位或Kerr参数优化);5)单参数Fisher分析表明高自旋情况下对ε的局域灵敏度提高;6)这些结果将圆轨道分支的反射性质与累积源相位效应联系起来,为引力波传播双折射检验提供补充途径。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了极端质量比旋进系统中,赤道反射对称性破缺通过二阶动力学效应的长期累积在引力波相位中产生可探测的偏差,特别是在快速旋转黑洞情况下。ε~10^-3量级的宇称破缺参数可能在未来空间引力波探测中被约束。然而,这些灵敏度估计依赖于绝热四极矩模型和特定比较方法,具有条件性。获得可靠的观测约束需要更精确的辐射建模(超越四极矩近似)和完整的参数估计分析。本工作为利用EMRI引力波信号检验黑洞时空对称性和修正引力理论提供了理论基础和可行性评估。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational-wave signatures of equatorial reflection symmetry breaking in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) around a parity-violating conformal Kerr black hole. For the circular-orbit branch connected to equatorial Kerr orbits, we derive the dynamics through second order in the dimensionless deformation parameter $\varepsilon$ and construct adiabatic inspirals and gravitational-wave signals using a mass-quadrupole prescription. Equatorial reflection maps $\varepsilon$ to $-\varepsilon$, making the polar displacement odd and the orbital frequency and energy even in the deformation. The induced first harmonic has a small direct contribution for the configurations studied, while second-order dynamical corrections accumulate over many cycles to produce appreciable dephasing. We find that rapid black-hole rotation enhances these phase effects and the associated noise-weighted waveform mismatch. For a one-year inspiral of a $10M_\odot$ object into a $10^6M_\odot$ black hole with $a/M=0.9$ and viewing inclination $ι=2π/3$, the fixed-parameter mismatch reaches the comparison benchmark $\mathcal M=0.01$ at approximately $\varepsilon=10^{-3}$, without optimization over time, phase, or Kerr parameters. A single-parameter Fisher analysis also indicates improved local sensitivity to $\varepsilon$ at high spin. These results connect the reflection properties of the circular branch to accumulated source-phase effects and complement tests of propagation birefringence. The sensitivity estimates remain conditional on the adiabatic quadrupole model and the adopted comparison procedure; robust observational constraints require more accurate radiation modeling and a full parameter-estimation analysis.

</details>


### [154] [Scattering of scalar waves by scalarized black holes in Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25316)
*Junyi Li,Yinrong Fang,Yang Huang,Qian Li,Junji Jia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内引力中标量化黑洞对大质量标量场的散射和吸收问题,对比了四次耦合和指数耦合两种模型,发现标量化抑制总吸收截面,且两种耦合在微分散射截面的角分布上呈现出可区分的特征模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论中,爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内(EsGB)引力允许黑洞发生标量化,即黑洞周围存在非平凡的标量场分布。为了探索标量化黑洞的可观测效应,并区分不同的耦合函数模型,需要系统研究标量场与这类黑洞的散射和吸收特性,这对于理解修正引力理论的物理预言和寻找可能的观测信号具有重要意义。

Method: 采用Chebyshev伪谱方法数值求解背景黑洞解,在纯数值黑洞背景上进行分波分析(partial-wave analysis)。研究了两种耦合模型:四次耦合f(φ)=α(φ²+βφ⁴)和指数耦合f(φ)=α(1-e^(-βφ²))。通过与测地线近似、glory近似以及Schwarzschild极限进行交叉验证,确保结果的可靠性。计算总吸收截面和微分散射截面,分析不同参数下的角分布特征。

Result: 标量化效应相对于Schwarzschild黑洞抑制了总吸收截面,并在微分散射截面中产生显著特征。两种耦合模型呈现出定性差异:四次耦合模型的偏离主要集中在中等角度区域(约70°-140°),在前向角和glory峰附近变化较小;指数耦合模型的偏离遍布全角度范围,显著改变glory峰,在某些参数区间甚至超过Schwarzschild值。分波结果与各种近似方法及Schwarzschild极限的一致性极好。

Conclusion: 标量化黑洞的散射特性与Schwarzschild黑洞存在可观测差异,且不同耦合函数模型在微分散射截面的角分布模式上具有特征性区别。四次耦合和指数耦合模型分别在特定角度区域表现出不同的偏离模式,这种独特的角度分布特征为通过散射观测量区分EsGB引力的耦合函数类型提供了潜在的诊断方法,对修正引力理论的观测检验具有重要价值。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the scattering and absorption of a massive scalar field by scalarized black holes in Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity with quartic coupling $f(φ)=α(φ^{2}+βφ^{4})$ and exponential coupling $f(φ)=α(1-\operatorname{e}^{-βφ^{2}})$. The background solutions are obtained numerically via a Chebyshev pseudospectral method, enabling a partial-wave analysis on purely numerical black hole backgrounds. The partial-wave results are cross-validated against geodesic and glory approximations and the Schwarzschild limit with excellent agreements. We find that scalarization suppresses the total absorption cross section relative to the Schwarzschild case, and produces distinctive signatures in the differential scattering cross section. A qualitative difference between the two coupling models is identified: for the quartic coupling model, deviations from Schwarzschild are concentrated in the intermediate-angle region $\sim(70^{\circ},140^{\circ})$, with comparatively small changes at forward angles and near the glory peak; for the exponential coupling model, deviations appear across the full angular range and substantially modify the glory peak, which in some parameter regimes can even exceed the Schwarzschild value. This distinctive angular pattern provides a potential diagnostic for distinguishing EsGB coupling functions from scattering observables.

</details>


### [155] [Interferometric trilemma for ultraviolet-softened gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25994)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了紫外软化的广义相对论扩展如何在短距离削弱引力,并通过三个干涉测量条件证明:若要求Källén-Lehmann谱密度为正,则引力强度不能低于牛顿引力。任何普适的短距离引力削弱都必然违反这三个条件之一,这为量子引力的紫外完备性提供了一致性检验标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的紫外扩展理论可以在短距离削弱引力强度,但正的Källén-Lehmann谱密度要求引力不能低于牛顿强度。这产生了一个基本问题:要实现引力削弱,必须牺牲介质的哪些性质?作者旨在通过干涉测量的角度,系统地识别出实现短距离引力削弱所必须违反的物理条件,从而为量子引力理论的紫外完备性提供可观测的一致性检验。

Method: 研究采用理论分析方法,针对与守恒应力张量线性耦合的洛伦兹不变高斯介质,提出了三个独立的干涉测量条件:(1)可见度永不被放大;(2)退相干由差分源辐射的能量固定;(3)没有干涉仪对后续干涉仪产生响应。然后在树图层次上,系统分析了二次引力理论中三种鬼场处方(负模鬼场、负能量鬼场和时间对称fakeon)如何各自精确违反其中一个条件,建立了引力削弱与条件违反之间的对应关系。

Result: 研究结果表明,二次引力的三种鬼场处方在树图层次各自违反一个干涉测量条件:负模鬼场放大相干性(违反条件1);负能量鬼场在没有从差分源正能量转移的情况下产生退相干(违反条件2);时间对称fakeon产生超前的条纹移动(违反条件3)。这证明了任何实现普适短距离引力削弱的理论,其介质必然违反所提出的三个条件之一。

Conclusion: 本文确立了短距离引力削弱与干涉测量条件违反之间的必然联系:任何在短距离普适削弱引力的理论都必须牺牲三个干涉测量条件中的至少一个。这一结果将原本用于证实量子引力的可观测量转化为检验其紫外完备性的一致性测试工具,为区分不同量子引力候选理论及其鬼场处理方案提供了可观测的判据,对量子引力理论的实验验证具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Ultraviolet-softened extensions of general relativity can weaken gravity at short distances, whereas a positive Källén--Lehmann spectral density forbids gravity from falling below Newtonian strength. Which property of the mediator must such a weakening sacrifice? For Lorentz-invariant Gaussian mediators coupled linearly to the conserved stress tensor, we formulate three independent interferometric conditions that jointly imply this bound: visibility is never amplified, decoherence is fixed by the energy radiated by the difference source, and no interferometer responds to a later one. At tree level, the three ghost prescriptions of quadratic gravity each violate exactly one: negative-norm ghosts amplify coherence, negative-energy ghosts decohere without positive energy transfer from the difference source, and a time-symmetric fakeon response produces advanced fringe shifts. Any universal short-distance weakening of gravity therefore certifies that its mediator violates one of these conditions, and the observables proposed to certify quantum gravity become consistency tests of its ultraviolet completion.

</details>


### [156] [Searching for lensed gravitational waves with unlensed templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26080)
*Digvijay Wadekar,Barak Zackay,Mark Ho-Yeuk Cheung,Tousif Islam,Javier Roulet,Ajit Kumar Mehta,Tejaswi Venumadhav,Jonathan Mushkin,Miguel Zumalacarregui,Matias Zaldarriaga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的引力透镜双像检测方法，通过重用未透镜模板的信噪比时间序列构建双像模板的匹配滤波输出，无需额外计算即可显著提升引力波搜索的灵敏度，速度比显式透镜模板库快150倍以上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力透镜效应会将双星并合信号分裂为两个像，这些像可能重叠干涉或作为独立事件到达。传统的未透镜模板无法充分捕获重叠透镜信号（仅能恢复67%的最优信噪比），且透镜信号会未通过信号一致性检验而被误判为噪声。需要一种计算高效且灵敏度更高的方法来检测透镜引力波信号

Method: 提出了一种双像方法，直接从现有搜索中已计算的未透镜模板信噪比时间序列构建双像模板的匹配滤波输出，无需额外的匹配滤波计算。该方法包括：(1)边缘化检测统计量用于重叠像检测；(2)相干搜索方法用于分离像的成对检测；(3)适用于点质量、奇异等温球、Chang-Refsdal和有核等温透镜等多种透镜模型

Result: 对于几何光学regime下的重叠像，该方法从点质量和奇异等温透镜中恢复≥97%的最优信噪比（相比未透镜模板的67%有大幅提升）。对于被显著降级的信号，灵敏体积提高约2-4倍。对于褶皱焦散线附近的分离像，灵敏体积比要求两个独立检测提高1.8-2.6倍，比目标对应体搜索提高1.6倍。计算速度比显式透镜模板库快超过150倍

Conclusion: 该双像方法能够高效地提升引力波搜索对透镜信号的探测灵敏度，既能恢复重叠像的信号一致性，又能实现分离像的相干搜索，且计算成本极低。该方法具有普适性，可应用于任何匹配滤波搜索，为引力透镜引力波事件的探测提供了实用且高效的解决方案

Abstract: Gravitational lensing can split the signal of a binary merger into two images, which either overlap and interfere or arrive as separate events. We show that the matched-filter output of every two-image template can be straightforwardly constructed from the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) time series that searches already compute with unlensed templates. This enables a marginalized detection statistic that increases search sensitivity without additional matched filtering and is more than 150 times faster than an explicit lensed template bank. For overlapping images in the geometric-optics regime, our two-image method recovers $\gtrsim97\%$ of the optimal SNR from point-mass and singular isothermal lenses, compared with as little as $67\%$ captured by unlensed templates, with substantial gains also for Chang-Refsdal and cored isothermal lenses. Overlapping lensed signals moreover fail the signal-consistency test that is used to down-rank noise transients. Our two-image method restores the consistency and increases the sensitive volume by roughly $2$-$4\times$ for the strongly down-ranked signals. For separated images, our method enables an efficient coherent search for pairs in which neither image is significant alone. Near fold caustics, this increases the sensitive volume by factors of 1.8-2.6 over requiring two independent detections, and up to 1.6 over a targeted counterpart search. Our method is applicable to any matched-filtering search.

</details>


### [157] [Black hole free energy landscapes from constrained instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26094)
*Shan-Ping Wu,Jia-Zhou Liu,Yu-Xiao Liu,Shao-Wen Wei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过引力路径积分因子化方法，为固定外部温度和角速度下的广义黑洞自由能景观提供了半经典解释，并将构造扩展到高阶曲率理论，在Einstein和高阶曲率引力中得到了统一的热力学形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞热力学研究缺乏对自由能景观的系统半经典解释，特别是在固定外部热力学参数（温度、角速度）条件下如何通过路径积分方法理解黑洞配分函数的鞍点结构。此外，需要将这种理解从Einstein引力推广到包含高阶曲率项的修正引力理论，以验证其普适性。

Method: 采用引力路径积分因子化技术，通过边界积分变换将配分函数表达为在辅助超曲面上带有完整局部偏Neumann数据的约束扇区积分。通过独立变分建立配对的正则子区域解作为约束瞬子，用Lagrange乘子支持时移和切向位移跳变以适应热力学和旋转失配。对于非极端稳态黑洞族，推导约束鞍点作用量形式$I_{\rm constr}=β\mathcal F_{\rm gen}=β(E_{\mathcal B}-Ω_{\mathcal B}^{I}J_{\mathcal B,I})-S$。进一步将构造扩展到局部宇称偶的$f(\mathrm{Riemann})$理论，通过保留完整辅助界面数据得到包含Wald熵的相同热力学形式。

Result: 成功建立了约束鞍点作用量与广义自由能的对应关系，其中$E_{\mathcal B}$和$J_{\mathcal B,I}$是重整化Hamilton荷，$S$是黑洞熵（来自正则视界帽的贡献）。自由能景观的内部驻点对应于光滑平衡鞍点，当保留的变分独立地跨越熵和角动量方向时成立。在高阶曲率理论中得到相同的热力学形式但使用Wald熵，无需小亏损展开。通过Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞验证Einstein构造，通过五维静态AdS黑洞（Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力）和旋转BTZ分支（新massive引力）明确检验了高阶曲率扩展。

Conclusion: 引力路径积分因子化方法为黑洞热力学提供了系统的半经典框架，成功将约束瞬子鞍点与广义自由能景观联系起来。该构造从Einstein引力自然推广到包含高阶曲率项的修正引力理论，熵贡献由Bekenstein-Hawking熵推广为Wald熵，保持了统一的热力学结构。多个经典黑洞解的显式验证证实了该方法的有效性和普适性，为理解量子引力中的黑洞热力学提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We apply gravitational path integral factorization to provide a semiclassical interpretation of generalized black hole free energy landscapes at fixed external temperature and angular velocities. Boundary integral transforms express the formal partition function as an integral over constrained sectors labeled by complete local partial-Neumann data on an auxiliary hypersurface. Independent variation establishes paired regular subregion solutions as constrained instantons, with Lagrange multipliers supporting lapse and tangential shift jumps that accommodate thermal and rotational mismatches. For selected nonextremal stationary families with an asymptotic boundary, the constrained saddle action takes the form $I_{\rm constr}=β\mathcal F_{\rm gen}=β(E_{\mathcal B}-Ω_{\mathcal B}^{I}J_{\mathcal B,I})-S$, with $β=T^{-1}$. Here $E_{\mathcal B}$ and $J_{\mathcal B,I}$ are renormalized Hamiltonian charges and $S$ is the black hole entropy, whose contribution to the action comes from the regular horizon cap. Interior stationary points of the landscape correspond to smooth equilibrium saddles when the retained variations independently span the entropy and angular momentum directions. We extend the construction to local, parity-even $f(\mathrm{Riemann})$ theories without curvature derivatives by retaining the complete auxiliary interface data. The same thermodynamic form follows with Wald entropy, without a small-deficit expansion. Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes illustrate the Einstein construction, while five-dimensional static AdS black holes in Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity and the rotating BTZ branch of new massive gravity provide explicit checks of the higher-curvature extension.

</details>


### [158] [Periodic orbits and gravitational-wave emission from extreme-mass-ratio inspirals around rotating charged black holes in nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26113)
*Xi Luo,Meirong Tang,Zhaoyi Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦引力理论框架下,研究了Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg非线性电动力学中旋转带电黑洞的测试粒子动力学和引力波特征,通过极端质量比旋近(EMRI)系统揭示了自旋和非线性电动力学电荷对轨道结构和引力波信号的影响,为LISA和"天琴"等空间引力波探测器检验无毛定理提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的无毛定理表明黑洞仅由质量、角动量和电荷三个物理量描述,但非线性电动力学(NED)黑洞可在视界附近发展出"毛发"结构,成为检验修正引力理论的理想试验场。现有研究缺乏对旋转带电NED黑洞在极端质量比旋近系统中测试粒子动力学和引力波特征的系统分析,而这对于空间引力波探测器(如LISA和天琴)区分NED黑洞时空并进行无毛定理的观测检验至关重要。

Method: 本研究采用爱因斯坦引力与非线性电动力学物质的耦合构造,聚焦于幂指数参数固定为s=2的Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg(EEH)变体。以自旋参数a和NED电荷q为主要控制变量,推导赤道面类时测地线和有效势,通过数值计算边界束缚轨道(MBO)和最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO),探索参数对能量-角动量(E-L)相空间的重塑作用。利用(z,w,v)整数值轨道分类法,通过绝热近似和四极辐射处方计算两个引力波极化模式h+和h×。

Result: 研究发现:(1)较大的自旋a和NED电荷q值驱动束缚轨道向内收缩,同时使E-L相空间向较小角动量值位移;(2)周期轨道的"变焦-回旋"(zoom-whirl)特征变得更加显著;(3)较大的参数值缩短引力波波形周期并增强信号振幅;(4)NED修正项对Kerr-Newman几何产生可观测的偏离效应,这些效应在EMRI配置的轨道动力学和引力波信号中均有体现。

Conclusion: 本文为Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg非线性电动力学框架下的旋转带电黑洞提供了完整的EMRI系统动力学和引力波辐射分析。研究结果表明,自旋和NED电荷参数对轨道结构和引力波信号产生显著且可区分的影响,为LISA和天琴等空间引力波天文台区分NED黑洞时空、对无毛定理进行观测检验提供了重要的理论预测和参考依据,有助于通过引力波观测探索修正引力理论和基本物理学。

Abstract: Within general-relativistic descriptions, the no-hair theorem dictates that black-hole geometries possess just three intrinsic physical quantities: mass, angular momentum, and electric charge. Black holes in nonlinear electrodynamics (NED) are equivalent to those composed of anisotropic matter and can develop "hair" structures near the event horizon, serving as ideal testbeds for modified theories of gravity. Working within Einsteinian gravitational theory, we adopt a coupling construction between gravity and NED matter, focusing explicitly on the Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg (EEH) variant with the power-index parameter fixed at s = 2. In this framework, a rotating, charged NED black hole extends the Kerr-Newman geometry with extra correction terms. Treating spin a and NED charge q as primary control variables, we examine test-particle dynamics inside extreme-mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) configurations. We derive equatorial timelike geodesics and the effective potential. Through numerical evaluation of marginally-bound orbits (MBO) and innermost stable circular orbits (ISCO), we explore how these parameters reshape the energy-angular-momentum (E-L) phase-space. Using the (z, w, v) integer-valued orbit taxonomy, we compute the two gravitational-wave polarization modes h+ and hx via the adiabatic approximation and quadrupole radiation prescription. Larger values for a and q drive inward contraction for bound trajectories while displacing the E-L phase-space toward smaller angular-momentum values. Periodic-orbit zoom-whirl signatures grow increasingly prominent, while larger parameter values shorten the waveform periods and enhance signal amplitudes. Our results provide theoretical support for space-borne gravitational-wave observatories including LISA and TianQin, enabling prospects for discriminating NED black-hole spacetimes and performing observational checks on the no-hair theorem.

</details>


### [159] [Local scale symmetry and black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26208)
*Malik Almatwi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了局域标度对称性下黑洞与实标量场耦合系统的解,分析了简单情况(λ=0)和一般情况(λ≠0)下黑洞的热力学性质、标量场的绝热吸积过程以及视界结构随参数的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局域标度对称性框架下黑洞与标量场耦合系统的性质,探索标量场对黑洞时空结构、热力学性质以及有效质量演化的影响,特别是在不同参数条件下系统呈现的不同物理行为。

Method: 采用局域标度对称性理论框架,首先求解简单情况(λ=0)下黑洞-标量场耦合系统的解析解,推导黑洞的温度、熵和有效质量;然后分析一般情况(λ≠0)下视界位置与黑洞质量、标量场强度、参数λ和正参数R_φ的依赖关系,研究标量场的空间分布特征。

Result: 在简单情况(λ=0)下,标量场向黑洞发生绝热吸积,导致黑洞有效质量增长;在一般情况(λ≠0)下,视界结构由多个参数共同决定,当黑洞质量较小时标量场主要集中在远离黑洞的区域,而当黑洞质量足够大时标量场主要集中在黑洞内部,系统呈现两种不同的物理行为模式。

Conclusion: 局域标度对称性框架下黑洞与标量场的耦合系统展现出丰富的物理行为:标量场的绝热吸积会导致黑洞有效质量增长;系统的视界结构和标量场空间分布强烈依赖于黑洞质量和耦合参数λ,在不同参数区间呈现质的差异,为理解标量场-黑洞相互作用提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We derive solutions for a black hole coupled to a real scalar field under considerations of local scale symmetry. Starting with the simple case $(λ=0)$, we find the solutions and derive the black hole's temperature, entropy, and its effective mass, and observe an adiabatic accretion of the scalar field onto the black hole. As a result, the effective mass of the black hole grows. In the general case $(λ\ne 0)$, the situation becomes more intricate; The horizons (the vanishing points of $g_{tt}$) depend on the mass of the black hole, scalar field strength, $λ$ and other positive parameter $R_φ$. In this case, we propose two possible behaviors: When the black hole's mass is small enough, the field is mostly concentrated far from the black hole, whereas when the black hole's mass is sufficiently large, it is mostly concentrated inside the black hole.

</details>


### [160] [Construction of Causal Set Propagators in $2+1$-Dimensional Constant-Curvature Spacetimes from Two-Link Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26221)
*Arsim Kastrati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在2+1维Minkowski、反de Sitter和de Sitter时空中因果集上基于链接的推迟标量传播子，发现其在高密度极限下与连续Klein-Gordon传播子一致，且呈现出有效曲率耦合系数ξ_link=1/6，这一普适值在不同维度中出现，暗示其可能反映了链接结构的紫外性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果集理论框架下构造物理场的传播子是连接离散时空结构与连续场论的关键问题。本研究旨在探索基于平方链接矩阵L²的标量传播子在2+1维不同曲率时空中的行为，特别是检验其连续极限是否能恢复标准的Klein-Gordon传播子，以及验证曲率耦合系数的普适性和物理起源。

Method: 研究采用因果集方法，将离散点随机撒播到2+1维Minkowski时空、反de Sitter时空和de Sitter时空中。核心构造基于平方链接矩阵L²，其期望值计算固定端点之间的两链路径数。在高密度极限下分析传播子的主导贡献，研究其在Alexandrov区间零边界交集附近的局域化行为，并检验平直空间归一化在常曲率时空中的不变性。通过与连续Klein-Gordon传播子的对比，提取有效曲率耦合参数。

Result: 在高密度极限下，基于链接的传播子的主导贡献局域化在Alexandrov区间的零边界交集附近。平直空间的归一化在常曲率时空中保持不变。得到的核函数与名义上无质量标量场的连续Klein-Gordon传播子一致,有效曲率耦合系数为ξ_link=1/6。这一数值不同于2+1维时空中的共形耦合值1/8,因此不是共形耦合效应。结合1+1维和3+1维的链接传播子结果(同样出现1/6系数),表明该耦合系数具有跨维度的普适性。

Conclusion: 基于链接矩阵的因果集传播子在2+1维不同曲率时空中成功恢复了连续Klein-Gordon传播子,有效曲率耦合系数ξ_link=1/6在不同时空维度(1+1、2+1、3+1维)中普遍出现。由于该值不等于各维度的共形耦合系数,研究认为这一耦合系数可能反映了链接结构本身的紫外(高能)性质,类似于局域热核展开中出现的(1/6-ξ)R项。这为理解因果集理论中离散结构如何在连续极限下产生有效场论提供了重要线索,揭示了时空离散性可能在量子场论紫外行为中扮演的角色。

Abstract: We study a link-based retarded scalar propagator on causal sets sprinkled into $2+1$-dimensional Minkowski, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter spacetimes. The construction is based on the squared link matrix $\mathbf{L}^2$, whose expectation value counts two-link paths between fixed endpoints. In the high-density limit, the dominant contribution localizes near the intersection of the null boundaries of the Alexandrov interval, and the flat-space normalization remains unchanged in constant curvature. The resulting kernels agree with the corresponding continuum Klein-Gordon propagators for a nominally massless scalar with effective curvature coupling $ξ_{\rm link}=1/6$. Since the conformal coupling in $2+1$ spacetime dimensions is $1/8$, this value is not a conformal-coupling effect. Together with link-based results in $1+1$ and $3+1$ dimensions, where the same coefficient $1/6$ appears, this suggests that the coupling may reflect an ultraviolet property of the link structure, reminiscent of the combination $(1/6-ξ)\mathcal{R}$ in local heat-kernel expansions.

</details>


### [161] [Geodesics of the five-dimensional Kerr-(A)dS black hole in closed form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26242)
*Özgür Sarıoğlu,Enes Yılmaz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文给出了五维Kerr-(A)dS黑洞中测地线运动Hamilton-Jacobi方程的通用闭形式解，采用Biermann-Weierstrass定理处理四次多项式，提供了对类时和类光测地线均适用的统一求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管五维Kerr-(A)dS黑洞中测地线运动的Hamilton-Jacobi方程在Boyer-Lindquist坐标系下可分离，但所得一阶系统此前只能根据径向和极角多项式的实根逐案积分求解，缺乏统一的通用解析解。需要发展一种不依赖于根结构分类、适用于所有情况的闭形式通用解。

Method: 采用无量纲变量将径向和极角多项式转化为四次多项式形式，应用Biermann-Weierstrass定理获得径向和极角坐标的一对显式实数公式。该参数化方法使用轨道上的任意点而非转折点作为参数，通过求积将时间坐标和两个方位角化简为第三类椭圆积分，仿射参数则通过额外的同类型积分获得。

Result: 成功构造了五维Kerr-(A)dS黑洞测地线运动的通用闭形式解，该解对类时和类光测地线均有效且不受根结构影响。所有坐标分量均可通过第三类椭圆积分表达。研究过程中还修正了已发表文献中的逆度规和径向势的错误。

Conclusion: 本文建立了五维Kerr-(A)dS黑洞测地线运动的完整解析框架，通过Biermann-Weierstrass定理实现了不依赖根分类的统一求解方案。该方法提供了显式实数形式的通解，所有时空坐标和仿射参数均可通过椭圆积分计算，为高维旋转黑洞时空中的测地线研究提供了系统的数学工具。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi equation for geodesic motion in the five-dimensional Kerr-(A)dS black hole separates in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates, but the resulting first-order system has so far been integrated case by case, according to the real roots of the radial and the polar polynomial. We give the general solution in closed form. In dimensionless variables both polynomials are quartic, and a theorem of Biermann and Weierstrass then yields the radial and the polar coordinate as one pair of manifestly real formulas, parametrized by an arbitrary point of the orbit rather than by a turning point, and valid for timelike and null geodesics alike whatever the root structure. The time coordinate and the two azimuthal angles follow by quadratures that reduce to elliptic integrals of the third kind alone, and the affine parameter by one more of the same type. Along the way we correct the inverse metric and the radial potential as published.

</details>


### [162] [Analog regular black holes and black hole mimickers in a Bose-Einstein condensate: from the core to the light rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26257)
*Valentin Pomakov,Stefano Liberati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何在准二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中模拟具有半径依赖质量函数的规则黑洞和黑洞模拟体,旨在实验室中探测内视界和内光环不稳定性。通过声学与引力线元的匹配,精确再现视界位置和表面引力,并推导出实现目标时空所需的外部势和相互作用强度的闭式表达式,提出了基于现有钾-39平台的三种可行实验设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力物理中的内视界和内光环不稳定性难以通过天体观测直接验证,需要在可控的实验室环境中进行模拟研究。玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体提供了一个理想的模拟平台,可以通过声学类比来再现黑洞时空的几何特性,从而在实验室尺度上探测这些基本的引力现象。

Method: 研究采用声学-引力对应方法,在准二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中构建模拟系统。通过在赤道盘上匹配声学和引力线元,固定径向流速与声速的比值,精确再现视界位置和表面引力。推导消除声速因子引起的光环位置偏移所需的相互作用剖面。结合度规对应关系与连续性方程和伯努利方程,获得实现完整目标时空所需的外部势和相互作用强度的闭式解,其中质量参数M和正则化参数ℓ可通过势的参数直接调控。最后将理论构造转化为具体的实验要求,并基于现有钾-39实验平台设计三种基准方案。

Result: 成功建立了玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体模拟规则黑洞的完整理论框架,获得了外部势和相互作用强度的闭式表达式,其中M和ℓ参数可直接调控。确定了实验实现所需的关键条件:准二维约束、愈合长度分辨率、三体寿命和稳态流寿命。提出了三种基于现有钾-39平台可实现的基准实验设计,证明了该方案在当前技术条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了利用准二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体模拟规则黑洞和黑洞模拟体的可行性,为在实验室中研究内视界和内光环不稳定性提供了切实可行的方案。通过声学-引力对应建立的理论框架不仅精确再现了目标时空的关键几何特性,而且所需的实验条件可在现有的钾-39平台上实现,为引力物理的模拟实验研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We show how static, spherically symmetric regular black holes and black-hole mimickers with radius-dependent mass function $m(r)$ can be emulated in a quasi-two-dimensional Bose--Einstein condensate, with the aim of probing inner-horizon and inner-light-ring instabilities in the laboratory. Matching the acoustic and gravitational line elements on an equatorial disc fixes the ratio of radial flow velocity to sound speed and reproduces both horizon locations and surface gravities exactly. The analogue geometry nevertheless contains a residual radially varying sound-speed factor in $g_{tt}$, which shifts the light-ring positions and reduces the range of the regularization parameter $\ell/M$ for which a light-ring pair exists. We derive the interaction profile that removes this shift. Combining the metric correspondence with the continuity and Bernoulli equations, we then obtain closed-form expressions for the external potential and interaction strength realizing the full target spacetime, with $M$ and $\ell$ directly tunable through parameters of the potential. Finally, we translate the construction into experimental requirements (quasi-two-dimensional confinement, healing-length resolution, three-body lifetime, and stationary-flow lifetime) and present three fiducial designs achievable with present-day $^{39}\mathrm{K}$ platforms.

</details>


### [163] [Outer trapped surface formation from gravitational waves collision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26320)
*Junbin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在无对称性假设的光滑真空时空中给出了外捕获面形成的新严格构造方法，通过两个横向局域化的近平面波前引力波碰撞产生外捕获面，并证明了最大未来发展的不完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Penrose利用Aichelburg-Sexl解构造两个超相对论粒子碰撞中边缘外捕获面的论证启发，本文旨在为光滑真空时空中的外捕获面形成提供严格的数学构造，无需依赖对称性假设，从而在更一般的物理情形下研究引力波碰撞导致的时空奇点形成问题。

Method: 采用短脉冲数据描述两个横向局域化的近平面波前引力波；通过沿曲线连接两个光滑片段构造外捕获面，确保其未来指向的外零法向量连续匹配；改编Penrose的原始论证方法建立最大未来发展的不完备性证明。整个构造过程不依赖对称性假设，在光滑真空时空框架下进行。

Result: 成功在无对称性假设的光滑真空时空中构造出外捕获面，该捕获面由引力波碰撞产生，具有连续匹配的未来指向外零法向量；证明了由此产生的最大未来发展存在不完备性，表明时空奇点的形成。

Conclusion: 本文为光滑真空时空中外捕获面的形成提供了新的严格数学构造，证明了在不依赖对称性假设的情况下，两个近平面波前引力波的碰撞可以产生外捕获面并导致时空不完备性。这一结果推广了Penrose的经典论证，为理解引力波碰撞中的奇点形成机制提供了更一般的数学框架，具有重要的理论意义。

Abstract: We give a new rigorous construction of outer trapped surface formation in smooth vacuum spacetimes without symmetry assumptions. The outer trapped surface is produced by the collision of two transversely localized, nearly plane-fronted gravitational waves described by short pulse data. The outer trapped surface is obtained by joining two smooth pieces along a curve with their future-directed outer null normals matching continuously. The incompleteness of the maximal future development is then established by adapting Penrose's original argument. This construction is inspired by Penrose's argument in constructing a marginally outer trapped surface in the collision of two ultra-relativistic particles modeled by the Aichelburg--Sexl solution.

</details>


### [164] [Primordial black holes in braneworld gravity: A gateway to hidden dimensions and the dark Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26482)
*Itzi Aldecoa-Tamayo*
