TL;DR: 本文研究了Randall-Sundrum II型膜世界场景中原初黑洞(PBHs)的宇宙学动力学,通过完全相对论性处理揭示了高维效应显著增强早期轻质PBHs的吸积过程,并重新评估了观测约束,识别出PBHs可完全解释暗物质的参数空间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四维时空框架下对原初黑洞演化的研究存在局限性。在高维膜世界情景(特别是Randall-Sundrum II型模型)中,早期宇宙的引力动力学会发生显著改变,但此前对PBHs在该场景下的吸积过程和演化历史缺乏完全相对论性的处理。同时,需要重新审视高维效应对PBHs作为暗物质候选者的观测约束影响,以确定其是否能够与现有观测数据相容并解释暗物质的全部组成。

Method: 第一部分采用Michel吸积理论结合协变的Shiromizu-Maeda-Sasaki形式体系,对早期宇宙中PBHs的吸积过程进行完全相对论性处理,量化分析高维效应对轻质黑洞早期吸积的影响。第二部分系统重新分析Randall-Sundrum宇宙学中高维PBHs面临的多重观测约束,包括:直接霍金辐射、未分辨的弥漫伽马射线背景、微引力透镜巡天数据,以及对宇宙微波背景的能量注入等,结合修正的高维蒸发动力学和最新观测数据进行综合评估。

Result: 研究发现高维效应显著增强了早期轻质PBHs的吸积率,导致黑洞质量与先前估计值的差异随宇宙时间按$t^{0.33}$规律增长,到强吸积时期结束时差异可达数个数量级。这从根本上改变了初始PBH质量谱与当前种群之间的映射关系。通过重新评估观测约束,识别出了参数空间中的可行区域,在该区域内PBHs可以完全解释暗物质的全部组成,同时与当前观测限制保持一致。特别值得注意的是,由于增强的吸积时期,能够构成暗物质显著部分的PBHs可能起源于低于有效四维普朗克尺度的质量。

Conclusion: 本论文提出了一种具有独特演化历史和当前观测特征的暗物质候选者。在Randall-Sundrum II型膜世界场景中,高维引力效应对原初黑洞的早期吸积和后续演化产生决定性影响,使得初始质量极小(甚至低于四维普朗克质量)的PBHs通过增强的吸积过程成长为可观的暗物质组分。研究确立了PBHs作为暗物质完整解释的可行性,并为通过观测验证该理论框架提供了明确的预测和约束条件。

Abstract: This thesis investigates the cosmological dynamics of primordial black holes (PBHs) within the Randall--Sundrum Type II braneworld scenario. The work comprises a preliminary discussion on gravitational collapse in the small-black-hole limit at early cosmological epochs, and two principal studies. The first study presents a fully relativistic treatment of PBH accretion in the early Universe, employing Michel accretion theory within the covariant Shiromizu--Maeda--Sasaki formalism. The analysis reveals that higher-dimensional effects substantially enhance early-time accretion onto light PBHs, with discrepancies between our black hole masses and previous estimates growing with cosmic time as $t^{0.33}$ --- a difference that reaches several orders of magnitude by the end of the strong-accretion epoch. This finding fundamentally alters the mapping between the initial PBH mass spectrum and present-day populations. The second study reassesses the observational and cosmological constraints on higher-dimensional PBHs in Randall--Sundrum cosmologies. Constraints from direct Hawking radiation, unresolved diffuse gamma-ray backgrounds, microlensing surveys, and energy injection into the cosmic microwave background are reanalysed in light of the modified higher-dimensional evaporation dynamics and updated observational surveys. Taken together, these results identify viable regions of parameter space in which PBHs can account for the entirety of dark matter while remaining consistent with current observational bounds. Notably, due to the enhanced accretion epoch, PBHs capable of constituting a significant fraction of dark matter may originate from masses below the effective four-dimensional Planck scale. This thesis thus puts forward a dark matter candidate with a distinct evolutionary history and observational signatures today.

</details>


### [165] [GPU-accelerated Bayesian inference for diffracted gravitational wave signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26619)
*Zihao Lin,Jef Heynen,Mick Wright,Thomas C. K. Ng,Chris Van Den Broeck,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文开发了Jim-lensing框架，首次实现了基于GPU加速的引力波透镜效应全波动光学贝叶斯推断，将点质量透镜模型下的参数估计时间从10小时以上缩短至约10分钟，为分析引力波透镜候选体和扩展透镜模型研究提供了高效计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波探测数量持续增长，观测到引力透镜引力波的概率增加。当透镜的史瓦西半径与引力波波长可比时，波动光学效应会产生频率依赖的信号调制。现有基于CPU的波动光学透镜参数估计计算密集度高，限制了对潜在透镜候选体显著性评估的研究规模，因此需要更高效的计算方法来处理引力波透镜分析。

Method: 在Jim（高性能GPU参数估计管道）中实现点质量透镜模型，构建Jim-lensing框架。该框架首次实现了GPU加速的全波动光学贝叶斯推断，包括贝叶斯因子计算，且无需训练数据。通过GPU并行计算能力加速参数估计过程，并与现有CPU管道进行性能和结果对比验证。

Result: Jim-lensing框架将点质量透镜假设下的完整参数估计运行时间从超过10小时缩短至约10分钟量级，相比现有最先进的CPU管道实现了显著加速。实验表明该实现稳定可靠，获得的结果与最先进的CPU管道具有可比性。

Conclusion: Jim-lensing框架相对于现有CPU管道的巨大速度优势为在更广泛的透镜模型下分析引力波数据铺平了道路，显著提升了产生稳健频率学派显著性估计的能力，为未来引力波透镜效应研究提供了强大的计算工具。

Abstract: As the rate of gravitational wave detections continues to grow, the probability of observing a gravitationally lensed gravitational wave also increases. When the Schwarzschild radius of the lens is comparable in size to the gravitational wave wavelength, wave-optics effects induce a frequency- dependent modulation on the signal. CPU-based parameter estimation analyses for wave-optics lensing are computationally intensive, limiting the scale of studies to determine the significance of any potential lensing candidate. In this work, we implement a point-mass lens model in Jim, a high-performance GPU-based parameter estimation pipeline. This enables, for the first time, GPU-accelerated Bayesian inference in full-wave optics, including the computation of Bayes factors, without the need for training data. We call this framework Jim-lensing. Using this framework, we are able to speed up a full parameter estimation run under the point-mass lens hypothesis from more than 10 hours to the order of 10 minutes compared to the current state-of-the-art CPU-based pipeline. We show that our implementation is stable and obtains comparable results to this state-of- the-art pipeline. The substantial speed gain over existing CPU pipelines paves the way for analyzing gravitational wave data under a broader range of lensing models with greater capacity to produce robust frequentist significance estimates.

</details>


### [166] [Cosmological perturbations from localized sources: a gauge-invariant multipolar theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26623)
*Geoffrey Compère,Sk Jahanur Hoque*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了空间平坦FLRW宇宙中局域源线性化引力场的第一性原理描述,推广了Blanchet-Damour多极形式体系,构建了包含所有多极阶和曲率尾效应的完整推迟度规,并在位置空间中直接获得规范不变多极矩,无需慢速运动或几何光学近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的引力波描述主要针对Minkowski时空或纯de Sitter时空,缺乏对任意膨胀历史的FLRW宇宙中引力场的完整第一性原理描述。标准的标量-矢量-张量分解需要通过源内部进行非局域空间投影,且难以处理曲率尾效应。需要建立一个适用于宇宙学背景、包含所有多极阶、规范不变且无需近似的引力场描述框架。

Method: 1) 推广Blanchet-Damour多极形式体系到FLRW时空;2) 在位置空间中直接构造规范不变的质量多极矩和流多极矩,避免通过源内部的非局域投影;3) 识别决定独立标量响应的正则宇宙流体矩;4) 建立完整的推迟度规构造,包含所有多极阶和曲率尾效应(光锥内源历史的贡献);5) 将正则多极度规与宇宙学微扰理论的标准变量关联;6) 对缓慢变化的亚哈勃尺度延展源定义有效多极源,通过截断笛卡尔多极矩实现。

Result: 成功构建了任意光滑膨胀历史FLRW宇宙中的完整推迟度规和宇宙流体微扰描述;获得了直接在位置空间表示的规范不变多极矩;发现标量响应在Minkowski和纯de Sitter时空中消失,但de Sitter时空中曲率尾效应仍然存在;恢复了已知的Minkowski和de Sitter多极场结果;恢复了早期de Sitter时空中的四极和八极模型;获得了包含辐射的ΛCDM模型中引力传播的解析表示。

Conclusion: 本文建立了空间平坦FLRW宇宙中局域源引力场的完整第一性原理描述框架,该框架具有规范不变性、包含完整的多极结构和曲率尾效应、无需慢速运动或几何光学近似,并可直接应用于ΛCDM等实际宇宙学模型。该方法为研究宇宙学背景下的引力波传播和源的多极辐射提供了严格的理论基础,推广并统一了Minkowski和de Sitter时空中的已有结果。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles description of the linearized gravitational field of localized sources in spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker universes with arbitrary smooth expansion histories. The construction determines the complete retarded metric and the induced perturbation of the surrounding cosmic fluid from prescribed source stresses and a cosmologically stationary past field. It includes all multipole orders and accounts for curvature tails, through which the field depends on the source's past history inside the light cone. Generalizing the Blanchet-Damour multipolar formalism, we identify gauge-invariant mass and current moments together with a canonical cosmic fluid moment that determines an independent scalar response. This response vanishes in Minkowski and pure de Sitter spacetime, although curvature tails persist in de Sitter. The gauge-invariant multipoles are obtained directly in position space. This gives an exterior description without the nonlocal spatial projection through the source interior required by the usual scalar-vector-tensor decomposition. We also relate the canonical multipole metric to the standard variables of cosmological perturbation theory. The retarded construction requires neither a slow-motion nor a geometric-optics approximation. We define effective multipolar sources by truncating the moments of slowly varying sub-Hubble extended sources at a prescribed Cartesian multipole order, recovering the earlier quadrupolar and octupolar models in de Sitter spacetime. We recover the known Minkowski and de Sitter multipolar fields and obtain an analytical representation for gravitational propagation in the $Λ$CDM model including radiation.

</details>


### [167] [A new class of rotating black hole solutions endowed with a scalar hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26678)
*Hugo Candan,Philippe Grandclement*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Horndeski标量-张量理论的子类中构建了新的毛发态稳态轴对称黑洞解。这些黑洞解通过谱方法数值获得,具有来自标量场线性时间依赖的初级毛发参数,偏离Kerr黑洞,且其非圆形Killing视界破坏了黑洞热力学第零定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论框架下,Kerr黑洞是唯一的真空稳态轴对称黑洞解(无毛定理)。但在修正引力理论中,特别是标量-张量理论中,可能存在携带额外标量场"毛发"的黑洞解。本研究旨在探索Horndeski理论(无非最小曲率耦合子类)中是否存在新的旋转毛发黑洞解,以及这些解的性质如何偏离经典Kerr时空。

Method: 采用基于谱方法的Kadath数值库求解完整的旋转黑洞解。关键技术包括:(1)不依赖显式ansatz,而是使用数值坐标系构建度规;(2)在Horndeski标量-张量理论的特定子类(无非最小曲率耦合)框架下求解Einstein场方程和标量场方程;(3)引入标量场的线性时间依赖项作为初级毛发参数;(4)通过谱方法数值求解稳态轴对称配置。

Result: 成功构建了一类新的稳态轴对称毛发黑洞数值解。这些解具有以下特征:(1)依赖于新的独立参数(初级毛发),源于标量场的线性时间依赖;(2)普遍偏离Kerr黑洞度规;(3)存在Killing视界,但视界是非圆形的;(4)破坏了黑洞热力学第零定律(即视界上表面引力不再为常数)。

Conclusion: 在Horndeski标量-张量理论的无非最小曲率耦合子类中,存在具有标量毛发的新型旋转黑洞解。这些解突破了广义相对论中的无毛定理限制,表现出非平凡的标量场分布和非圆形视界结构。黑洞热力学第零定律的破坏表明,在修正引力理论中黑洞的热力学性质可能发生根本改变,为检验广义相对论和探索强引力场中的新物理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present new hairy stationary and axisymmetric black hole solutions. These solutions belong to a subclass of Horndeski scalar-tensor theories, in which there is no non-minimal coupling to curvature. The full rotating solutions are obtained numerically using the Kadath library based on spectral methods. The metric does not rely on an explicit ansatz but rather on the use of a set of numerical coordinates. Black holes obtained differ from Kerr in general, and depend on a new independent parameter, a primary hair, coming from a linear time dependence in the scalar field. These solutions admit a Killing horizon like the Kerr black hole, but are non-circular, and break the zeroth law of black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [168] [Gravitational fingerprints of a black hole solution in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26692)
*Carlos F. S. Pereira,Araújo Filho,Marcos V. de S. Silva,N. Heidari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力理论中静态黑洞的粒子运动、光学观测量和线性波传播特性,发现在固定轨道质量下许多关键物理量保持Schwarzschild值,但径向形变参数σ会影响固有距离、下落时间、潮汐效应和引力波准简正模频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力理论框架下,需要系统研究静态黑洞的物理性质,特别是区分保守质量M与轨道质量M̄的影响,并分析径向形变参数σ对各种观测量的作用,以检验该修正引力理论与观测数据(如Sgr A*和M87*黑洞阴影)的兼容性。

Method: 采用理论分析与数值计算相结合的方法:1)分析测地线运动,计算光子球、临界冲击参数和最内稳定圆轨道;2)研究径向自由落体的固有时间和潮汐效应;3)计算弱场和强场regime下的光偏折角,考虑圆锥渐近特性;4)利用射线追踪方法计算黑洞阴影并与观测数据比较;5)对标量场、电磁场、无质量Dirac场和引力扰动进行线性摄动分析;6)使用三阶WKB近似和时间演化方法计算准简正模频率。

Result: 在固定轨道质量M̄时,光子球半径、临界冲击参数和最内稳定圆轨道保持Schwarzschild值,径向频率比也不变;但固有距离、下落时间和累积轨道角依赖于σ。径向下落的潮汐本征值包含因子σ^(-2),光子轨道不稳定性指数按σ^(-1)标度。黑洞阴影与Sgr A*和M87*的比较显示存在耦合简并性。齐次轴向引力扰动扇区(δB_μν=0)与Schwarzschild谱精确映射,振荡率和衰减率均按σ^(-1)标度。三阶WKB估计与时间演化提取的基模频率符合度在0.7%以内。

Conclusion: Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力中的静态黑洞在轨道质量固定时,许多观测量保持Schwarzschild特征,但径向形变参数σ系统性地影响时空几何相关的物理量(固有距离、潮汐效应)和动力学过程(准简正模频率)。黑洞阴影观测存在参数简并,需要结合引力波准简正模等其他观测手段打破简并。该理论的齐次轴向引力扰动扇区与Schwarzschild黑洞保持谱映射关系,为检验修正引力理论提供了可观测窗口。

Abstract: We investigate particle motion, optical observables, and linear wave propagation around a static black hole in Palatini Kalb Ramond gravity with a vanishing cosmological constant. The analysis distinguishes the conserved mass $M$ from the orbital mass $\bar{M}=M/ρ$ and isolates the radial deformation $σ^2=1-q/2$, where $q=\ell_2/(1-\ell_1)$. At fixed $\bar{M}$, the photon sphere, critical impact parameter, and innermost stable circular orbit retain their Schwarzschild values. Radial frequency ratios also preserve their Schwarzschild profiles for the same areal release and particle radii, while proper distances, infall times, and accumulated orbital angles depend on $σ$. For radial fall, the tidal eigenvalues acquire a factor $σ^{-2}$, and the instability exponent of the photon orbit scales as $σ^{-1}$. In addition, we examine light deflection in the weak and strong regimes with an explicit account of the conical asymptotics. The shadow comparison with Sgr A* and M87* exhibits a coupling degeneracy when the orbital mass and areal distance are set. Scalar, electromagnetic, and massless Dirac perturbations probe the physical metric, whereas a homogeneous axial gravitational sector with $δB_{μν}=0$ admits an exact Schwarzschild spectral map. In this sector, both oscillation and attenuation rates scale as $σ^{-1}$ at fixed $\bar{M}$. Third order WKB estimates agree with frequencies extracted from time evolution to within $0.7\%$ for the reported fundamental modes.

</details>


### [169] [Spectroscopy as a Probe of Spacetime Foam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26766)
*A. G. Kim,P. E. Nugent,L. Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究量子引力时空泡沫对远源光线的影响,提出通过光谱畸变探测时空泡沫时间变化的新方法,并利用GRB 221009A的MeV发射线和宇宙微波背景辐射(CMB)光谱对泡沫指数α进行约束,得到α≳0.52-0.63的下限,这些结果与全息值α=2/3一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要通过成像通道约束时空泡沫的空间变化,缺乏对其时间变化的有效探测手段。量子引力时空泡沫会在光传播过程中引入相位退相干和随机到达时间抖动,这些效应会改变光的时间相干性和观测光谱。因此需要发展互补的光谱学方法来探测时空泡沫的时间动力学特性,并对泡沫指数α进行更严格的约束。

Method: 研究时空泡沫引起的相位抖动对一阶时间相干函数g^(1)(τ)的影响,推导出在动力学泡沫极限下光谱分裂为相干线芯(被e^(-σ_φ^2)因子抑制)和非相干基座的特征。基于此提出三种光谱探测方法:(1)宽带流量的整体存活性;(2)谱线的分数宽度;(3)宽带连续谱的色散亏损。将理论应用于GRB 221009A的MeV发射线和COBE/FIRAS的CMB单极光谱数据,通过拟合光谱畸变对泡沫指数α进行约束,并预测下一代光谱仪的探测能力。

Result: 从GRB 221009A的MeV发射线获得α≳0.63的弱约束(主要受杠杆臂效应限制)。对COBE/FIRAS的CMB单极光谱分析在95%置信水平下得到α≳0.52,预测下一代光谱仪仅能达到α≳0.54-0.55(受泡沫-温度简并性限制)。基于谱线宽度和CMB的约束给出低于全息值α=2/3的下限,与GRB 221009A成像窗口一致且不排除该窗口的任何部分。条件性TeV存活界(α≳0.74)仅在近普朗克刷新率泡沫(τ_foam≲4×10^(-28)s)情况下成立,该条件会排除成像窗口。

Conclusion: 光谱畸变为探测量子引力时空泡沫的时间变化提供了有效的互补方法。当前观测数据对泡沫指数α的光谱学约束(α≳0.52-0.63)构成理论下限,与全息预言α=2/3相容。这些与基座无关的约束结果与GRB 221009A成像观测一致,仅有条件性的TeV存活界在极端泡沫刷新率假设下会与成像窗口产生矛盾。光谱方法受系统简并性限制,但为理解量子引力效应提供了重要的观测窗口。

Abstract: Quantum-gravitational spacetime foam varying in space and in time imprints, respectively, a transverse phase decoherence and an achromatic, stochastic arrival-time jitter on light from distant sources. We show that the jitter modifies the first-order temporal coherence $g^{(1)}(τ)$ and hence the observed spectrum, providing a spectroscopic probe of the temporal variation, complementary to existing imaging-channel constraints on the spatial variation. In the dynamical-foam limit, where the foam decorrelates within the light's coherence time, the spectrum splits into a coherent line core suppressed by $e^{-σ_φ^2}$, with $σ_φ^2$ the variance of the foam-induced phase jitter, and an incoherent pedestal, yielding a spectral shape-distortion bound on the foam exponent $α$. Three probes follow: the outright survival of broadband flux, the fractional width of a spectral line, and a chromatic deficit across a broadband continuum. Applied to the MeV emission lines of GRB\,221009A we obtain $α\gtrsim 0.63$, a weak, lever-arm-dominated floor. For the broadband CMB the foam induces an $e^{-σ_φ^2(ν)}$ deficit ($σ_φ^2 \propto ν^2$); a fit to the COBE/FIRAS monopole spectrum reaches $α\gtrsim 0.52$ ($95\%$ CL), and next-generation spectrometers reach only $α\gtrsim 0.54$--$0.55$ (forecast), limited by the foam--temperature degeneracy. The pedestal-independent line-width and CMB bounds are floors below the holographic value $α= 2/3$, consistent with and excluding no part of the imaging window reported for GRB\,221009A; only the conditional TeV survival bound ($α\gtrsim0.74$), which holds only for near-Planck-refresh foam ($τ_{\rm foam}\lesssim4\times10^{-28}$\,s), would exclude it --- and, with it, the window.

</details>


### [170] [Spherically symmetric solutions with dust and radiation sources in Schouten-Codazzi Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26775)
*Sebastián Nájera,Roberto A. Sussman,Juan Carlos Hidalgo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schouten-Codazzi引力(SCG)理论中的精确解,这是一种在Einstein场方程中加入Codazzi张量的二阶度规理论。通过分析广义Vaidya和Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi时空,发现该理论可在不引入暗能量的情况下解释宇宙加速膨胀,为宇宙学应用提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论需要引入"暗能量"来解释宇宙加速膨胀等现象。本文旨在探索Schouten-Codazzi引力理论作为广义相对论的替代或扩展方案,通过在Einstein场方程中添加Codazzi张量来研究是否能够在不引入暗能量的情况下描述宇宙学现象,从而为引力理论提供更丰富的动力学框架。

Method: 本文采用精确解分析方法研究SCG理论。具体包括:1)在Einstein场方程中引入满足Codazzi方程的Codazzi张量;2)考察两种球对称几何:描述零辐射的广义Vaidya时空和描述非均匀尘埃的Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi(LTB)时空;3)通过Codazzi方程对时空几何施加约束,由两个积分函数确定,这些函数自然生成有效"宇宙学常数"和额外自由度;4)分析Codazzi贡献对Misner-Sharp质量的修正以及LTB模型的演化方程。

Result: 研究发现:1)对于Vaidya几何,Codazzi贡献修改了Misner-Sharp质量;2)LTB模型的演化方程是一个三次多项式,其系数依赖于Codazzi积分函数;3)该理论产生了比广义相对论更广泛的动力学类别;4)在不引入"暗能量"的情况下,该理论允许加速膨胀和永久膨胀模型的存在。

Conclusion: Schouten-Codazzi引力理论为宇宙学应用提供了一个有吸引力的理论框架。通过在Einstein场方程中引入Codazzi张量,该理论能够在不依赖暗能量假设的前提下解释宇宙加速膨胀等现象,展现出比广义相对论更丰富的动力学行为,为理解宇宙演化提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: We examine in this paper exact solutions of Schouten-Codazzi gravity (SCG), a second order metric theory in which a Codazzi tensor is added to the Einstein field equations. We consider two spherically symmetric geometries that describe null radiation and inhomogeneous dust, corresponding to generalized Vaidya and Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi (LTB) spacetimes. The Codazzi tensor has to comply the Codazzi equations, this imposes restrictions on the geometry of spacetime that are determined up to two integration functions that naturally generate an effective ``cosmological constant'' and an additional degree of freedom. We show that for the Vaidya geometry, these Codazzi contributions modify the Misner-Sharp mass, while the LTB models have as an evolution equation a cubic polynomial whose coefficients depend on the Codazzi integration functions. This leads to a broader class of dynamics than those of General Relativity, and allows for an accelerated expansion and ever-expanding models without the introduction of ``dark energy''. Overall, our analysis suggests that Schouten-Codazzi gravity provides an appealing framework for applications in cosmology.

</details>
